内部 ID 7105（JD001 铁令求和）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    ll a = read(), b = read();
    printf("%lld\n", a + b);
}
 
signed main(){
 //   std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7106（JD002 铁令相乘）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    ll a = read(), b = read();
    printf("PROD = %lld\n", a * b);
}
 
signed main(){
 //   std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7107（JD003 四令求差）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    ll a = read(), b = read(), c = read(), d = read();
    printf("DIFFERENCE = %lld\n", a * b - c * d);
}
 
signed main(){
 //   std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7108（JD004 集市算账）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    int id = read();
    ll num = read();
    double a; std::cin >> a;
    printf("TOTAL = %.2lf\n", num * a);
}
 
signed main(){
 //   std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7109（JD005 比武台）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    double r; std::cin >> r;
    printf("A=%.4lf\n", r * r * 3.14159);
}
 
signed main(){
 //   std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    double a, b; std::cin >> a >> b;
    printf("Average = %.5lf\n", (a * 3.5 + b * 7.5) / 11.0);
}
 
signed main(){
 //   std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

#include <map>
#include <set>

    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;

ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}

        a = (a * a) % p;

void solve(){

    return f ? x : -x;

ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}

    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    double x; std::cin >> x;
    if(x >= 0 and x <= 25) puts("Interval [0,25]");
    else if(x > 25 and x < 50) puts("Interval (25,50]");
    else if(x > 50 and x < 75) puts("Interval (50,75]");
    else if(x > 75 and x <= 100) puts("Interval (75,100]");
    else puts("Out of interval");
}
 
signed main(){
 //   std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7127（JD023 账房调薪）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    double salary;
    cin >> salary;

    int p; // 涨幅百分比（整数）
    if (salary <= 400.0) p = 15;
    else if (salary <= 800.0) p = 12;
    else if (salary <= 1200.0) p = 10;
    else if (salary <= 2000.0) p = 7;
    else p = 4;

    double increase = salary * p / 100.0;
    double newSalary = salary + increase;

    cout << fixed << setprecision(2);
    cout << "New salary: " << newSalary << "\n";
    cout << "Increase: " << increase << "\n";
    cout << "Percentage: " << p << " %\n";  // 注意空格
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7130（JD026 木棍辨形）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;
 
int main() {
    double sides[3];
    cin >> sides[0] >> sides[1] >> sides[2];
 
    // 降序排列，确保 sides[0] 为最长边
    sort(sides, sides + 3, greater<double>());
    double a = sides[0], b = sides[1], c = sides[2];
 
    // 1. 是否能构成三角形
    if (a >= b + c) {
        cout << "Not a triangle" << endl;
        return 0;
    }
 
    // 2. 角类型（根据最长边平方与另外两边平方和的关系）
    double a2 = a * a, b2 = b * b, c2 = c * c;
    if (fabs(a2 - (b2 + c2)) < 1e-9)
        cout << "Right" << endl;
    else if (a2 > b2 + c2)
        cout << "Obtuse" << endl;
    else
        cout << "Acute" << endl;
 
    // 3. 边类型（等腰/等边）
    if (fabs(a - b) < 1e-9 && fabs(b - c) < 1e-9)
        cout << "Equilateral" << endl;
    else if (fabs(a - b) < 1e-9 || fabs(b - c) < 1e-9 || fabs(a - c) < 1e-9)
        cout << "Isosceles" << endl;
    // 不等边不输出额外行
 
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7131（JD027 怪兽辨识）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
#include <string>

using namespace std;

int main()
{
    string a, b, c;

    cin >> a >> b >> c;

    if (a == "vertebrado")
    {
        if (b == "ave")
        {
            if (c == "carnivoro")
                cout << "aguia";
            else
                cout << "pomba";
        }
        else if (b == "mamifero")
        {
            if (c == "onivoro")
                cout << "homem";
            else
                cout << "vaca";
        }
    }
    else if (a == "invertebrado")
    {
        if (b == "inseto")
        {
            if (c == "hematofago")
                cout << "pulga";
            else
                cout << "lagarta";
        }
        else if (b == "anelideo")
        {
            if (c == "hematofago")
                cout << "sanguessuga";
            else
                cout << "minhoca";
        }
    }

    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7132（JD028 田赋计算）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    double x;
    cin >> x;

    if (x <= 2000.0) {
        cout << "Isento" << endl;
        return 0;
    }

    double tax = 0.0;
    // 区间 (2000, 3000] 按 8%
    if (x > 3000.0) tax += 1000.0 * 0.08;
    else tax += (x - 2000.0) * 0.08;

    // 区间 (3000, 4500] 按 18%
    if (x > 4500.0) tax += 1500.0 * 0.18;
    else if (x > 3000.0) tax += (x - 3000.0) * 0.18;

    // 区间 (4500, +∞) 按 28%
    if (x > 4500.0) tax += (x - 4500.0) * 0.28;

    cout << fixed << setprecision(2) << "R$ " << tax << endl;
    return 0;
}

#include <map>
#include <set>

#include <stack>

    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    int n = read();
    std::vector<int> a(n);
    int cnt = 0;
    for(auto& x : a){
        x = read();
        if(x >= 10 and x <= 20) ++cnt;
    }
    printf("%d in\n%d out\n", cnt, n - cnt);
}
 
signed main(){
 //   std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7140（JD036 奇数求和）

#include <map>

#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    ll sum = 0;
    ll x = read(), y = read();
    for(ll i = min(x, y) + 1; i < max(x, y); ++i){
        if(i & 1) sum += i;
    }
    printf("%lld\n", sum);
}
 
signed main(){

  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();

}

    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;

//ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}

    std::vector<int> a;
    int x;

内部 ID 7146（JD042 兵刃统计）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    int N;
    cin >> N;

    int total = 0;
    int cntC = 0, cntR = 0, cntF = 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        int num;
        char type;
        cin >> num >> type;
        total += num;
        if (type == 'C') cntC += num;
        else if (type == 'R') cntR += num;
        else if (type == 'F') cntF += num;
    }

    cout << "Total: " << total << " weapons\n";
    cout << "Total swords: " << cntC << "\n";
    cout << "Total blades: " << cntR << "\n";
    cout << "Total spears: " << cntF << "\n";

    cout << fixed << setprecision(2);
    cout << "Percentage of swords: " << (cntC * 100.0 / total) << " %\n";
    cout << "Percentage of blades: " << (cntR * 100.0 / total) << " %\n";
    cout << "Percentage of spears: " << (cntF * 100.0 / total) << " %\n";

    return 0;
}

    int a[20];
    for (int i = 0; i < 20; ++i) cin >> a[i];

内部 ID 7151（JD047 剑锋所指）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：Python3 ｜ JSON

import sys

def main():
    data = sys.stdin.read().strip().split()
    if not data:
        return

    n = int(data[0])
    nums = list(map(int, data[1:1+n]))
    if n == 0:
        return
    min_val = min(nums)
    pos = nums.index(min_val)  # 返回第一个最小值的索引
    print(f"Menor valor: {min_val}")
    print(f"Posicao: {pos}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

内部 ID 7152（JD048 二气相生）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    int N;
    cin >> N;

    long long a = 0, b = 1;   // F(0) 和 F(1)
    if (N == 0) {
        cout << a << endl;
        return 0;
    }
    if (N == 1) {
        cout << b << endl;
        return 0;
    }

    for (int i = 2; i <= N; ++i) {
        long long c = a + b;
        a = b;
        b = c;
    }
    cout << b << endl;
    return 0;
}

#include <cassert>

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
//ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}

    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}

std::vector<ull> fib(80);

void pre(){
    fib[0] = 0, fib[1] = 1;
    for(int i = 2; i < 80; ++i) fib[i] = fib[i-1] + fib[i-2];
}

void solve(){
    int n = read();
    for(int i = 0; i < n; ++i) printf("%llu ", fib[i]);
    puts("");
}

  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    pre();
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7154（JD050 列阵求和）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long
#pragma GCC optimize("O2")
#include <cassert>

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
//ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}

void solve(){
    ll n, m;
    while(std::cin >> n >> m){
        if(!n and !m or n < 0 or m < 0) break;
        ll sum = 0;
        for(ll i = min(n, m); i <= max(n, m); ++i) printf("%lld ", i), sum += i;
        printf("Sum=%lld\n", sum);
    }
}
 
signed main(){

  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7155（JD051 剑行取道）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
#include <iomanip>

using namespace std;

int main()
{
    int L;
    char op;

    cin >> L;
    cin >> op;

    double a[12][12];

    for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++)
    {
        for (int j = 0; j < 12; j++)
        {
            cin >> a[i][j];
        }
    }

    double sum = 0;

    for (int j = 0; j < 12; j++)
    {
        sum += a[L][j];
    }

    if (op == 'M')
    {
        sum /= 12;
    }

    cout << fixed << setprecision(1) << sum << endl;

    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7156（JD052 剑列纵横）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    int C;
    char op;
    cin >> C >> op;

    double sum = 0.0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 12; ++i) {
        for (int j = 0; j < 12; ++j) {
            double x;
            cin >> x;
            if (j == C) sum += x;
        }
    }

    double ans = (op == 'S') ? sum : sum / 12.0;
    cout << fixed << setprecision(1) << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}

ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}

std::vector<int> primes, minp;

void sieve(int n){
    minp.resize(n+1);
    for(int i = 2; i <= n; ++i){
        if(!minp[i]){
            minp[i] = i;
            primes.push_back(i);
        }
        for(auto j : primes){
            if(j > minp[i] or j > n / i) break;
            minp[i*j] = j;
        }
    }
}

void solve(){
    int n = read();
    sieve(n);
    for(auto& p : primes) printf("%d\n", p);
}
 
signed main(){
 //   std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7167（JD063 方圆初阵）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    int N;
    while (cin >> N && N != 0) {
        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
            for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
                int val = min(min(i + 1, j + 1), min(N - i, N - j));
                cout << setw(3) << val;
            }
            cout << endl;
        }
        cout << endl;   // 每个矩阵后输出一个空行
    }
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7168（JD064 镜像方阵）

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    int N;
    while (cin >> N && N != 0) {
        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
            for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
                cout << setw(3) << abs(i - j) + 1;
            }
            cout << endl;
        }
        cout << endl;   // 每个矩阵后跟一个空行
    }
    return 0;
}

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    int N;
    while (cin >> N && N != 0) {
        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
            for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
                cout << (1 << (i + j)) << " ";
            }
            cout << endl;
        }
        cout << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7170（JD066 蛇形剑阵）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long
#define re register

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}

ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}

void solve(){
    int n = read(), m = read();
    const int dx[] = {0, 1, 0, -1}, dy[] = {1, 0, -1, 0};
    std::vector<std::vector<int>> a(n + 1, std::vector<int>(m + 1, 0));
    
    int x = 1, y = 1, dir = 0;
    for(int cnt = 1; cnt <= n * m; ++cnt){
        a[x][y] = cnt;
        int nx = x + dx[dir], ny = y + dy[dir];
        if(nx < 1 or nx > n or ny < 1 or ny > m or a[nx][ny]){
            dir = (dir + 1) % 4;
            nx = x + dx[dir], ny = y + dy[dir];
        }
        x = nx, y = ny;
    }

    for(int i = 1; i <= n; ++i){
        for(int j = 1; j <= m; ++j){
            printf("%d ", a[i][j]);
        } puts("");
    }
}

int main(){
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long
#pragma GCC optimize("O2")
#include <cassert>

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
//ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}

void solve(){
    std::string s; std::getline(std::cin, s);
    for(auto& ch : s) putchar(ch), putchar(32);
}
 
signed main(){
 //   std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7173（JD069 墨迹遮字）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    string s;
    char ch;

    getline(cin, s);   // 读入字符串
    cin >> ch;         // 读入要替换的字符

    for (char &c : s) {
        if (c == ch) c = '#';
    }

    cout << s << endl;
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7174（JD070 经文重构）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    string a;
    getline(cin, a);
    int n = a.size();

    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        int sum = (unsigned char)a[i] + (unsigned char)a[(i + 1) % n];
        cout << (char)sum;
    }
    cout << endl;
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7176（JD072 拳掌对决）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <cstdio>
#include <iostream>

using namespace std;

int main()
{
    int n = 1;

    while (n -- )
    {
        string a, b;
        cin >> a >> b;

        int x, y;
        if (a == "Hunter") x = 0;
        else if (a == "Bear") x = 1;
        else x = 2;

        if (b == "Hunter") y = 0;
        else if (b == "Bear") y = 1;
        else y = 2;

        if (x == y) puts("Tie");
        else if (x == (y + 1) % 3) puts("Player1");
        else puts("Player2");
    }

    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7177（JD073 经文加密）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long
#pragma GCC optimize("O2")
#include <cassert>

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
//ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}

void solve(){
    std::string s; std::getline(std::cin, s);
    for(auto& ch : s){
      /*  if(isdigit(ch)){
            if(ch != '9') ch += 1;
            else ch = '0';
        }*/
        if(isalpha(ch)){
            if(ch != 'z' and ch != 'Z') ch += 1;
            else if(ch == 'z') ch = 'a';
            else if(ch == 'Z') ch = 'A';
        }
    }
    std::cout << s << std::endl;
}
 
signed main(){
 //   std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7178（JD074 古卷替换）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    string text;
    getline(cin, text);
    string from, to;
    cin >> from >> to;

    stringstream ss(text);
    vector<string> words;
    string word;
    while (ss >> word) {
        if (word == from) word = to;
        words.push_back(word);
    }

    for (int i = 0; i < (int)words.size(); ++i) {
        if (i) cout << " ";
        cout << words[i];
    }
    cout << endl;
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7179（JD075 寻最长词）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    string s;
    getline(cin, s);
    
    // 去掉末尾的句号
    if (!s.empty() && s.back() == '.') s.pop_back();

    stringstream ss(s);
    string word, longest;
    while (ss >> word) {
        if (word.length() > longest.length()) longest = word;
    }
    cout << longest << endl;
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7180（JD076 古卷插字）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    string str, sub;
    while (cin >> str >> sub) {
        int pos = 0;
        char maxChar = str[0];
        for (int i = 1; i < (int)str.size(); ++i) {
            if (str[i] > maxChar) {
                maxChar = str[i];
                pos = i;
            }
        }
        str.insert(pos + 1, sub);
        cout << str << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7181（JD077 独字寻踪）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    string s;
    cin >> s;
    int cnt[26] = {0};
    for (char c : s) cnt[c - 'a']++;
    for (char c : s) {
        if (cnt[c - 'a'] == 1) {
            cout << c << endl;
            return 0;
        }
    }
    cout << "no" << endl;
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7183（JD079 经文周期）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    string s;
    while (cin >> s && s != ".") {
        int n = s.size();
        for (int p = 1; p <= n; ++p) {
            if (n % p == 0) {
                bool ok = true;
                for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
                    if (s[i] != s[i % p]) {
                        ok = false;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (ok) {
                    cout << n / p << endl;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7184（JD080 密文跨距）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    string s;
    getline(cin, s);
    vector<string> parts;
    stringstream ss(s);
    string item;
    while (getline(ss, item, ',')) {
        parts.push_back(item);
    }
    if (parts.size() < 3) return 0;
    string S = parts[0], S1 = parts[1], S2 = parts[2];
    size_t pos1 = S.find(S1);
    size_t pos2 = S.rfind(S2);
    if (pos1 == string::npos || pos2 == string::npos || pos1 + S1.length() > pos2) {
        cout << -1 << endl;
    } else {
        int distance = pos2 - (pos1 + S1.length());
        cout << distance << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7190（JD086 剑阵复制）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    int n = read(), m = read(), k = read();
    for(int i = 1; i <= n; ++i){
        if(i <= k) printf("%d ", read());
        else read();
    }
    for(int i = 1; i <= m; ++i){
        if(i <= k) read();
        else printf("%d ", read());
    }
}
 
signed main(){
 //   std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7191（JD087 剑阵翻转）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    int n = read(), k = read();
    std::vector<int> a(n);
    for(auto& x : a) x = read();
    std::reverse(a.begin(), a.begin() + k);
    for(auto& x : a) printf("%d ", x);
}

内部 ID 7192（JD088 印数成招）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    int n = read(), k = read();
    for(int i = 0; i < k; ++i) printf("%d ", read());
}
 
signed main(){
 //   std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7193（JD089 印阵成招）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>

using namespace std;

void print2D(int a[][100], int row, int col)
{
    for (int i = 0; i < row; i ++ )
    {
        for (int j = 0; j < col; j ++ )
            cout << a[i][j] << ' ';
        cout << endl;
    }
}

int main()
{
    int a[100][100];

    int row, col;

    cin >> row >> col;
    for (int i = 0; i < row; i ++ )
        for (int j = 0; j < col; j ++ )
            cin >> a[i][j];

    print2D(a, row, col);

    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7194（JD090 剑阵排序）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long
 
ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    int n = read(), l = read(), r = read();
    std::vector<int> a(n);
    for(auto& x : a) x = read();
    std::sort(a.begin() + l, a.begin() + r + 1);
    for(auto& x : a) printf("%d ", x);
}
 
signed main(){
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7198（JD094 方阵寻路）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long
 
ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    int n = read(), m = read();
    ll res = 1;
    for(int i = 0; i < n; ++i) res *= (n + m - i);
    for(int i = 0; i < n; ++i) res /= (i + 1);
    printf("%lld\n", res);
}
 
signed main(){
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7199（JD095 全排列阵）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long
 
ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    int n = read();
    std::vector<int> a(n);
    std::iota(a.begin(), a.end(), 1);
    do{
        printf("%d", a[0]);
        for(int i = 1; i < n; ++i) printf(" %d", a[i]);
        puts("");
    } while(std::next_permutation(a.begin(), a.end()));
}
 
signed main(){
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);

内部 ID 7203（JD099 双栈为队）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}

ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}

void solve(){
    std::queue<int> q;
    std::string op;
    while(std::cin >> op){
        if(op == "empty") puts(q.empty() ? "yes" : "no");
        if(op == "push") q.push(read());
        if(op == "pop"){
            printf("%d\n", q.front()); q.pop();
        }
    }
}

signed main(){
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7206（JD102 三元归序）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}

ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}

void solve(){
    struct X {
        int x; double y; std::string z;
    };
    int n = read();
    std::vector<X> v(n);
    for(auto& [x, y, z] : v) std::cin >> x >> y >> z;
    std::sort(v.begin(), v.end(), [](const X& a, const X& b){
        if(a.x != b.x) return a.x < b.x;
        return a.y < b.y;
    });

    for(auto& [x, y, z] : v){
        printf("%d %.2lf %s\n", x, y, z.c_str());
    }
}

signed main(){
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long
#pragma GCC optimize("O2")
#include <cassert>
 
ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
//ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
int a[1000005];
 
void solve(){
    int n; std::cin >> n;
    for(int i = 0; i < n; ++i) std::cin >> a[i];
    std::stable_sort(&a[0], &a[n]);
    for(int i = 0; i < n; ++i) printf("%d ", a[i]);
}
 
signed main(){
    std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7208（JD104 照妖辨品）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
#include <cstring>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cmath>
using namespace std;
 
const int N = 100010;
 
int a[N];
 
int quick_sort(int q[], int l, int r, int k)
{
    //同快速排序
    int x = q[(l + r) / 2], i = l - 1, j = r + 1;
    if (l >= r) return q[l];
    while (i < j)
    {
        while (q[ ++ i] < x);
        while (q[ -- j] > x);
        if (i < j) swap(q[i], q[j]);
    }
    if (j - l + 1 >= k) return quick_sort(q, l, j, k); //第k小数在左边区间
    else return quick_sort(q, j + 1, r, k - (j - l + 1)); //第k小数在右边区间
}
 
int main()
{
    int n, k;
    scanf("%d%d", &n, &k);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i ++ ) scanf("%d", &a[i]);
    printf("%d", quick_sort(a, 1, n, k));
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7210（JD106 逆流之数）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
#define int long long
#define MAXN 500005
 
int n, inverse_cnt;
int a[MAXN], b[MAXN];
 
int read(){
    int x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(;!isdigit(ch);ch = getchar()) if(ch == '-') f = -1;
    for(;isdigit(ch);ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch xor 48);
    return x*f;
}
 
void merge_sort(int l, int r){
    if(l >= r) return;
    int mid = (l + r) >> 1;
    merge_sort(l, mid); merge_sort(mid + 1, r);
    int i = l, j = mid + 1, tot = 0;
    while(i <= mid and j <= r){
        if(a[j] < a[i]){
            b[++tot] = a[j++];
            inverse_cnt += mid - i + 1;
        }
        else b[++tot] = a[i++];
    }
    while(i <= mid) b[++tot] = a[i++];
    while(j <= r) b[++tot] = a[j++];
    for(int k = l; k <= r; ++k) a[k] = b[k - l + 1];
}
 
signed main(){
    n = read();
    for(int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) a[i] = read();
    merge_sort(1, n);
    printf("%lld\n", inverse_cnt);
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7211（JD107 石壁听风）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long
#pragma GCC optimize("O2")
#include <cassert>
 
ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
//ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    int n, q; std::cin >> n >> q;
    std::vector<int> a(n);
    for(int i = 0; i < n; i++) std::cin >> a[i];
    while(q--){
        int x; std::cin >> x;
        int L = std::lower_bound(a.begin(), a.end(), x) - a.begin();
        int R = std::upper_bound(a.begin(), a.end(), x) - a.begin() - 1;
        if(L > R) puts("-1 -1");
        else printf("%d %d\n", L, R);
    }
}
 
signed main(){
    std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7212（JD108 剑指方根）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <numeric>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long
#pragma GCC optimize("O2")
#include <cassert>
 
ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}
 
//ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    while(b){
        if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
        a = (a * a) % p;
        b >>= 1;
    }
    return res;
}
 
void solve(){
    double x; std::cin >> x;
    double L = -10000.0, R = 10000.0;
    while(R - L > 1e-8){
        double mid = (L + R) / 2.0;
        double y = mid * mid * mid;
        if(y < x) L = mid;
        else R = mid;
    }
    printf("%.6lf\n", L);
}
 
signed main(){
    std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
  //  std::cin.tie(0);
  //  std::cout.tie(0);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7213（JD109 铁壁识痕）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
using namespace std;
inline int lowbit(int &x)
{
    return x & -x;//x的二进制的最后一个1所构成的整数
}
int main()
{
    int n, x;
    cin >> n;
    while (n--)
    {
        //读入一个数，并且计算1的个数，并且输出
        cin >> x;
        int res = 0;//计数变量
        while (x)
            x -= lowbit(x), res++; //使用,表达式，简化代码去括号
        cout << res << " ";        //输出运算结果
    }
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7214（JD110 万钧合一）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iomanip>
#include <numeric>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long
#ifdef LOCAL
    #include "debug.h"
#else
    #define debug(...) (__VA_ARGS__)
    #define dbg(...) (__VA_ARGS__)
#endif
#pragma GCC optimize("Ofast")

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}

ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    for(; b; b >>= 1, a = (a * a) % p) if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
    return res;
}

namespace BigInt {
    const int base = 1000000000;
    const int base_digits = 9; // 分解为九个数位一个数字
    struct BigInt {
        std::vector<int> a;
        int sign;

        #pragma region Constructors

        BigInt() : sign(1) {}
        BigInt(const BigInt&) = default;
        BigInt(BigInt&&) noexcept = default;
        BigInt& operator = (const BigInt&) = default;
        BigInt& operator = (BigInt&&) noexcept = default;

        explicit BigInt(ll v){*this = v;}
        explicit BigInt(int v){*this = (ll)v;}
        BigInt(const std::string& s){str2int(s);}

        #pragma endregion
        #pragma region Utils
        
        void trim(){
            while(!a.empty() and !a.back()) a.pop_back();
            if(a.empty()) sign = 1;
        }

        bool isZero() const {
            return a.empty() or (a.size() == 1 and !a[0]);
        }

        BigInt abs() const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res.sign = 1;
            return res;
        }

        explicit operator bool() const {
            return !isZero();
        }

        void str2int(const std::string& s){
            sign = 1;
            a.clear();
            int pos = 0, n = s.size();
            for(; pos < n and isspace(s[pos]); ++pos);
            for(; pos < n and (s[pos] == '+' or s[pos] == '-'); ++pos) if(s[pos] == '-') sign = -1;
            for(; pos < n and s[pos] == '0'; ++pos);
            if(pos == n){a.clear(); sign = 1; return;}
            for(int i = n - 1; i >= pos; i -= base_digits){
                int x = 0;
                for(int j = std::max(pos, i - base_digits + 1); j <= i; ++j) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (s[j] xor 48);
                a.push_back(x);
            }
            trim();
        }

        std::string to_string() const {
            if(isZero()) return "0";
            std::string s = (sign == -1 ? "-" : "");
            s += std::to_string(a.back());
            for(int i = (int)a.size() - 2; i >= 0; --i){
                std::string t = std::to_string(a[i]);
                s += std::string(base_digits - t.size(), '0') + t;
            }
            return s;
        }

        ll to_ll() const {
            ll res = 0;
            for(int i = a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i) res = res * base + a[i];
            return res * sign;
        }

        static std::vector<int> convert_base(const std::vector<int>& a, int old_digits, int new_digits){
            std::vector<ll> p(std::max(old_digits, new_digits) + 1);
            p[0] = 1;
            for(int i = 1; i < (int)p.size(); ++i) p[i] = p[i - 1] * 10;
            std::vector<int> res;
            ll cur = 0;
            int cur_digits = 0;
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a.size(); ++i){
                cur += (ll)a[i] * p[cur_digits];
                cur_digits += old_digits;
                while(cur_digits >= new_digits){
                    res.push_back((int)(cur % p[new_digits]));
                    cur /= p[new_digits];
                    cur_digits -= new_digits;
                }
            }
            if(cur > 0) res.push_back(cur);
            while(!res.empty() and !res.back()) res.pop_back();
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator - () const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res.sign = -res.sign;
            return res;
        }

        void operator = (ll v){
            a.clear();
            sign = 1;
            if(v < 0) sign = -1, v = -v;
            for(; v; v = v / base) a.push_back(v % base);
        }

        void swap(BigInt& other) noexcept {
            a.swap(other.a);
            std::swap(sign, other.sign);
        }

        #pragma endregion
        #pragma region Arithmetics

        BigInt operator + (const BigInt& v) const {
            if(sign != v.sign) return *this - (-v);
            BigInt res = v;
            ll carry = 0;
            int n = std::max(a.size(), v.a.size());
            res.a.resize(n, 0);
            for(int i = 0; i < n or carry; ++i){
                if(i == res.a.size()) res.a.push_back(0);
                ll av = (i < a.size() ? a[i] : 0);
                ll sum = res.a[i] + av + carry;
                res.a[i] = (int)(sum % base);
                carry = sum / base;
            }
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator - (const BigInt& v) const {
            if(sign != v.sign) return *this + (-v);
            if(abs() < v.abs()) return -(v - *this);
            BigInt res = *this;
            ll borrow = 0;
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)v.a.size() or borrow; ++i){
                ll sub = (ll)res.a[i] - (i < v.a.size() ? v.a[i] : 0) - borrow;
                if(sub < 0) sub += base, borrow = 1;
                else borrow = 0;
                res.a[i] = (int)sub;
            }
            res.trim();
            return res;
        }

        void operator += (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this + v;
        }

        void operator -= (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this - v;
        }

        void operator *= (int v){
            operator *= ((ll)v);
        }

        void operator *= (ll v){
            if(v < 0) sign = -sign, v = -v;
            ll carry = 0;
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a.size() or carry; ++i){
                if(i == a.size()) a.push_back(0);
                ll cur = a[i] * v + carry;
                carry = cur / base;
                a[i] = cur % base;
            }
            trim();
        }

        void operator /= (int v){
            operator /= ((ll)v);
        }

        void operator /= (ll v){
            if(v < 0) sign = -sign, v = -v;
            ll rem = 0;
            for(int i = (int)a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i){
                ll cur = a[i] + rem * base;
                a[i] = cur / v;
                rem = cur % v;
            }
            trim();
        }

        ll operator % (int v) const {
            return operator % ((ll)v);
        }

        ll operator % (ll v) const {
            if(v < 0) v = -v;
            ll m = 0;
            for(int i = (int)a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i){
                m = (m * (ll)base + a[i]) % v;
            }
            return m * sign;
        }

        void operator %= (int v){
            *this = *this % v;
        }

        void operator %= (ll v){
            *this = *this % v;
        }

        BigInt operator * (int v) const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res *= v;
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator * (ll v) const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res *= v;
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator / (int v) const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res /= v;
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator / (ll v) const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res /= v;
            return res;
        }

        bool operator < (const BigInt& v) const {
            if(sign != v.sign) return sign < v.sign;
            if(a.size() != v.a.size()) return a.size() * sign < v.a.size() * v.sign;
            for(int i = a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
                if(a[i] != v.a[i]) return a[i] * sign < v.a[i] * v.sign;
            return false;
        }

        bool operator > (const BigInt& v) const {
            return v < *this;
        }

        bool operator <= (const BigInt& v) const {
            return !(v < *this);
        }

        bool operator >= (const BigInt& v) const {
            return !(*this < v);
        }

        bool operator == (const BigInt& v) const {
            return !(*this < v) and !(v < *this);
        }

        bool operator != (const BigInt& v) const {
            return  (*this < v) or  (v < *this);
        }

        friend BigInt gcd(BigInt a, BigInt b){
            while(!b.isZero()){
                BigInt r = a % b;
                a = std::move(b);
                b = std::move(r);
            }
            return a;
        }

        friend BigInt lcm(const BigInt& a, const BigInt& b){
            if(a.isZero() or b.isZero()) return BigInt(0);
            return a / gcd(a, b) * b;
        }

        friend BigInt pow(BigInt a, ll e){
            BigInt res(1);
            for(; e; e >>= 1, a = a * a) if(e & 1) res *= a;
            return res;
        }

        friend BigInt pow(BigInt _base, BigInt exp, const BigInt& mod){
            BigInt base = _base % mod;
            BigInt res(1);
            while(!exp.isZero()){
                if((exp % 2) != 0) res = (res * base) % mod;
                base = (base * base) % mod;
                exp /= 2;
            }
            return res % mod;
        }

        #pragma endregion
        #pragma region IO

        friend std::istream& operator >> (std::istream &stream, BigInt& v){
            std::string s; stream >> s;
            v.str2int(s);
            return stream;
        }

        friend std::ostream& operator << (std::ostream &stream, const BigInt& v){
            if(v.sign == -1) stream << "-";
            stream << (v.a.empty() ? 0 : v.a.back());
            for(int i = (int)v.a.size() - 2; i >= 0; --i)
                stream << std::setw(base_digits) << std::setfill('0') << v.a[i];
            return stream;
        }

        #pragma endregion
        #pragma region MulDiv

        #define vll std::vector<ll>

        BigInt operator * (const BigInt& v) const {
            std::vector<int> a6 = convert_base(a, base_digits, 6);
            std::vector<int> b6 = convert_base(v.a, base_digits, 6);
            vll a(a6.begin(), a6.end());
            vll b(b6.begin(), b6.end());
            while(a.size() < b.size()) a.push_back(0);
            while(b.size() < a.size()) b.push_back(0);
            while(a.size() & (a.size() - 1)) a.push_back(0), b.push_back(0);
            vll c = Karatsuba(a, b);
            BigInt res;
            res.sign = sign * v.sign;
            ll carry = 0;
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)c.size(); ++i){
                ll cur = c[i] + carry;
                res.a.push_back(cur % 1000000);
                carry = cur / 1000000;
            }
            while(carry){
                res.a.push_back(carry % 1000000);
                carry /= 1000000;
            }
            res.a = convert_base(res.a, 6, base_digits);
            res.trim();
            return res;
        }

        static vll Karatsuba(const vll& a, const vll& b){
            int n = a.size();
            vll res(n << 1);
            if(n <= 32){
                for(int i = 0; i < n; ++i){
                    for(int j = 0; j < n; ++j){
                        res[i + j] += a[i] * b[j];
                    }
                }
                return res;
            }
            int k = n >> 1;
            vll a1(a.begin(), a.begin() + k);
            vll a2(a.begin() + k, a.end());
            vll b1(b.begin(), b.begin() + k);
            vll b2(b.begin() + k, b.end());

            vll a1b1 = Karatsuba(a1, b1);
            vll a2b2 = Karatsuba(a2, b2);
            
            for(int i = 0; i < k; ++i) a2[i] += a1[i];
            for(int i = 0; i < k; ++i) b2[i] += b1[i];
            
            vll r = Karatsuba(a2, b2);
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a1b1.size(); ++i) r[i] -= a1b1[i];
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a2b2.size(); ++i) r[i] -= a2b2[i];
            
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)r.size(); ++i) res[i + k] += r[i];
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a1b1.size(); ++i) res[i] += a1b1[i];
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a2b2.size(); ++i) res[i + n] += a2b2[i];
            return res;
        }

        friend std::pair<BigInt, BigInt> divmod(const BigInt& a1, const BigInt& b1){
            int norm = base / (b1.a.back() + 1);
            BigInt a = a1.abs() * norm;
            BigInt b = b1.abs() * norm;
            BigInt q, r;
            q.a.resize(a.a.size());
            for(int i = (int)a.a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i){
                r *= base;
                if(r.a.empty()) r.a.push_back(a.a[i]);
                else r.a[0] += a.a[i];
                r.trim();
                int s1 = r.a.size() <= b.a.size() ? 0 : r.a[b.a.size()];
                int s2 = r.a.size() <= b.a.size() - 1 ? 0 : r.a[b.a.size() - 1];
                ll d = ((ll)base * s1 + s2) / b.a.back();
                if(d > base - 1) d = base - 1;
                BigInt bd = b * d;
                while(r < bd){
                    --d;
                    bd = b * d;
                }
                r = r - bd;
                q.a[i] = d;
            }
            q.sign = a1.sign * b1.sign;
            r.sign = a1.sign;
            q.trim(); r.trim();
            if(norm != 1) r /= norm;
            return std::make_pair(q, r);
        }

        void operator *= (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this * v;
        }

        BigInt operator / (const BigInt& v) const {
            return divmod(*this, v).first;
        }

        void operator /= (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this / v;
        }

        BigInt operator % (const BigInt& v) const {
            return divmod(*this, v).second;
        }

        void operator %= (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this % v;
        }

        #undef vll
        #pragma endregion
    };
};

void solve(){
    BigInt::BigInt a, b;
    std::cin >> a >> b;
    std::cout << a + b << std::endl;
}

signed main(){
    std::cin.tie(0) -> std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7215（JD111 削铁如泥）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>

#include <cmath>

#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <climits>

#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long
#ifdef LOCAL
    #include "debug.h"
#else
    #define debug(...) (__VA_ARGS__)
    #define dbg(...) (__VA_ARGS__)
#endif
#pragma GCC optimize("Ofast")

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}

ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    for(; b; b >>= 1, a = (a * a) % p) if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
    return res;
}

namespace BigInt {
    const int base = 1000000000;
    const int base_digits = 9; // 分解为九个数位一个数字
    struct BigInt {
        std::vector<int> a;
        int sign;

        #pragma region Constructors

        BigInt() : sign(1) {}
        BigInt(const BigInt&) = default;
        BigInt(BigInt&&) noexcept = default;
        BigInt& operator = (const BigInt&) = default;
        BigInt& operator = (BigInt&&) noexcept = default;

        explicit BigInt(ll v){*this = v;}
        explicit BigInt(int v){*this = (ll)v;}
        BigInt(const std::string& s){str2int(s);}

        #pragma endregion
        #pragma region Utils
        
        void trim(){
            while(!a.empty() and !a.back()) a.pop_back();
            if(a.empty()) sign = 1;
        }

        bool isZero() const {
            return a.empty() or (a.size() == 1 and !a[0]);
        }

        BigInt abs() const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res.sign = 1;
            return res;
        }

        explicit operator bool() const {
            return !isZero();
        }

        void str2int(const std::string& s){
            sign = 1;
            a.clear();
            int pos = 0, n = s.size();
            for(; pos < n and isspace(s[pos]); ++pos);
            for(; pos < n and (s[pos] == '+' or s[pos] == '-'); ++pos) if(s[pos] == '-') sign = -1;
            for(; pos < n and s[pos] == '0'; ++pos);
            if(pos == n){a.clear(); sign = 1; return;}
            for(int i = n - 1; i >= pos; i -= base_digits){
                int x = 0;
                for(int j = std::max(pos, i - base_digits + 1); j <= i; ++j) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (s[j] xor 48);
                a.push_back(x);
            }
            trim();
        }

        std::string to_string() const {
            if(isZero()) return "0";
            std::string s = (sign == -1 ? "-" : "");
            s += std::to_string(a.back());
            for(int i = (int)a.size() - 2; i >= 0; --i){
                std::string t = std::to_string(a[i]);
                s += std::string(base_digits - t.size(), '0') + t;
            }
            return s;
        }

        ll to_ll() const {
            ll res = 0;
            for(int i = a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i) res = res * base + a[i];
            return res * sign;
        }

        static std::vector<int> convert_base(const std::vector<int>& a, int old_digits, int new_digits){
            std::vector<ll> p(std::max(old_digits, new_digits) + 1);
            p[0] = 1;
            for(int i = 1; i < (int)p.size(); ++i) p[i] = p[i - 1] * 10;
            std::vector<int> res;
            ll cur = 0;
            int cur_digits = 0;
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a.size(); ++i){
                cur += (ll)a[i] * p[cur_digits];
                cur_digits += old_digits;
                while(cur_digits >= new_digits){
                    res.push_back((int)(cur % p[new_digits]));
                    cur /= p[new_digits];
                    cur_digits -= new_digits;
                }
            }
            if(cur > 0) res.push_back(cur);
            while(!res.empty() and !res.back()) res.pop_back();
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator - () const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res.sign = -res.sign;
            return res;
        }

        void operator = (ll v){
            a.clear();
            sign = 1;
            if(v < 0) sign = -1, v = -v;
            for(; v; v = v / base) a.push_back(v % base);
        }

        void swap(BigInt& other) noexcept {
            a.swap(other.a);
            std::swap(sign, other.sign);
        }

        #pragma endregion
        #pragma region Arithmetics

        BigInt operator + (const BigInt& v) const {
            if(sign != v.sign) return *this - (-v);
            BigInt res = v;
            ll carry = 0;
            int n = std::max(a.size(), v.a.size());
            res.a.resize(n, 0);
            for(int i = 0; i < n or carry; ++i){
                if(i == res.a.size()) res.a.push_back(0);
                ll av = (i < a.size() ? a[i] : 0);
                ll sum = res.a[i] + av + carry;
                res.a[i] = (int)(sum % base);
                carry = sum / base;
            }
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator - (const BigInt& v) const {
            if(sign != v.sign) return *this + (-v);
            if(abs() < v.abs()) return -(v - *this);
            BigInt res = *this;
            ll borrow = 0;
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)v.a.size() or borrow; ++i){
                ll sub = (ll)res.a[i] - (i < v.a.size() ? v.a[i] : 0) - borrow;
                if(sub < 0) sub += base, borrow = 1;
                else borrow = 0;
                res.a[i] = (int)sub;
            }
            res.trim();
            return res;
        }

        void operator += (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this + v;
        }

        void operator -= (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this - v;
        }

        void operator *= (int v){
            operator *= ((ll)v);
        }

        void operator *= (ll v){
            if(v < 0) sign = -sign, v = -v;
            ll carry = 0;
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a.size() or carry; ++i){
                if(i == a.size()) a.push_back(0);
                ll cur = a[i] * v + carry;
                carry = cur / base;
                a[i] = cur % base;
            }
            trim();
        }

        void operator /= (int v){
            operator /= ((ll)v);
        }

        void operator /= (ll v){
            if(v < 0) sign = -sign, v = -v;
            ll rem = 0;
            for(int i = (int)a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i){
                ll cur = a[i] + rem * base;
                a[i] = cur / v;
                rem = cur % v;
            }
            trim();
        }

        ll operator % (int v) const {
            return operator % ((ll)v);
        }

        ll operator % (ll v) const {
            if(v < 0) v = -v;
            ll m = 0;
            for(int i = (int)a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i){
                m = (m * (ll)base + a[i]) % v;
            }
            return m * sign;
        }

        void operator %= (int v){
            *this = *this % v;
        }

        void operator %= (ll v){
            *this = *this % v;
        }

        BigInt operator * (int v) const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res *= v;
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator * (ll v) const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res *= v;
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator / (int v) const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res /= v;
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator / (ll v) const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res /= v;
            return res;
        }

        bool operator < (const BigInt& v) const {
            if(sign != v.sign) return sign < v.sign;
            if(a.size() != v.a.size()) return a.size() * sign < v.a.size() * v.sign;
            for(int i = a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
                if(a[i] != v.a[i]) return a[i] * sign < v.a[i] * v.sign;
            return false;
        }

        bool operator > (const BigInt& v) const {
            return v < *this;
        }

        bool operator <= (const BigInt& v) const {
            return !(v < *this);
        }

        bool operator >= (const BigInt& v) const {
            return !(*this < v);
        }

        bool operator == (const BigInt& v) const {
            return !(*this < v) and !(v < *this);
        }

        bool operator != (const BigInt& v) const {
            return  (*this < v) or  (v < *this);
        }

        friend BigInt gcd(BigInt a, BigInt b){
            while(!b.isZero()){
                BigInt r = a % b;
                a = std::move(b);
                b = std::move(r);
            }
            return a;
        }

        friend BigInt lcm(const BigInt& a, const BigInt& b){
            if(a.isZero() or b.isZero()) return BigInt(0);
            return a / gcd(a, b) * b;
        }

        friend BigInt pow(BigInt a, ll e){
            BigInt res(1);
            for(; e; e >>= 1, a = a * a) if(e & 1) res *= a;
            return res;
        }

        friend BigInt pow(BigInt _base, BigInt exp, const BigInt& mod){
            BigInt base = _base % mod;
            BigInt res(1);
            while(!exp.isZero()){
                if((exp % 2) != 0) res = (res * base) % mod;
                base = (base * base) % mod;
                exp /= 2;
            }
            return res % mod;
        }

        #pragma endregion
        #pragma region IO

        friend std::istream& operator >> (std::istream &stream, BigInt& v){
            std::string s; stream >> s;
            v.str2int(s);
            return stream;
        }

        friend std::ostream& operator << (std::ostream &stream, const BigInt& v){
            if(v.sign == -1) stream << "-";
            stream << (v.a.empty() ? 0 : v.a.back());
            for(int i = (int)v.a.size() - 2; i >= 0; --i)
                stream << std::setw(base_digits) << std::setfill('0') << v.a[i];
            return stream;
        }

        #pragma endregion
        #pragma region MulDiv

        #define vll std::vector<ll>

        BigInt operator * (const BigInt& v) const {
            std::vector<int> a6 = convert_base(a, base_digits, 6);
            std::vector<int> b6 = convert_base(v.a, base_digits, 6);
            vll a(a6.begin(), a6.end());
            vll b(b6.begin(), b6.end());
            while(a.size() < b.size()) a.push_back(0);
            while(b.size() < a.size()) b.push_back(0);
            while(a.size() & (a.size() - 1)) a.push_back(0), b.push_back(0);
            vll c = Karatsuba(a, b);
            BigInt res;
            res.sign = sign * v.sign;
            ll carry = 0;
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)c.size(); ++i){
                ll cur = c[i] + carry;
                res.a.push_back(cur % 1000000);
                carry = cur / 1000000;
            }
            while(carry){
                res.a.push_back(carry % 1000000);
                carry /= 1000000;
            }
            res.a = convert_base(res.a, 6, base_digits);
            res.trim();
            return res;
        }

        static vll Karatsuba(const vll& a, const vll& b){
            int n = a.size();
            vll res(n << 1);
            if(n <= 32){
                for(int i = 0; i < n; ++i){
                    for(int j = 0; j < n; ++j){
                        res[i + j] += a[i] * b[j];
                    }
                }
                return res;
            }
            int k = n >> 1;
            vll a1(a.begin(), a.begin() + k);
            vll a2(a.begin() + k, a.end());
            vll b1(b.begin(), b.begin() + k);
            vll b2(b.begin() + k, b.end());

            vll a1b1 = Karatsuba(a1, b1);
            vll a2b2 = Karatsuba(a2, b2);
            
            for(int i = 0; i < k; ++i) a2[i] += a1[i];
            for(int i = 0; i < k; ++i) b2[i] += b1[i];
            
            vll r = Karatsuba(a2, b2);
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a1b1.size(); ++i) r[i] -= a1b1[i];
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a2b2.size(); ++i) r[i] -= a2b2[i];
            
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)r.size(); ++i) res[i + k] += r[i];
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a1b1.size(); ++i) res[i] += a1b1[i];
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a2b2.size(); ++i) res[i + n] += a2b2[i];
            return res;
        }

        friend std::pair<BigInt, BigInt> divmod(const BigInt& a1, const BigInt& b1){
            int norm = base / (b1.a.back() + 1);
            BigInt a = a1.abs() * norm;
            BigInt b = b1.abs() * norm;
            BigInt q, r;
            q.a.resize(a.a.size());
            for(int i = (int)a.a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i){
                r *= base;
                if(r.a.empty()) r.a.push_back(a.a[i]);
                else r.a[0] += a.a[i];
                r.trim();
                int s1 = r.a.size() <= b.a.size() ? 0 : r.a[b.a.size()];
                int s2 = r.a.size() <= b.a.size() - 1 ? 0 : r.a[b.a.size() - 1];
                ll d = ((ll)base * s1 + s2) / b.a.back();
                if(d > base - 1) d = base - 1;
                BigInt bd = b * d;
                while(r < bd){
                    --d;
                    bd = b * d;
                }
                r = r - bd;
                q.a[i] = d;
            }
            q.sign = a1.sign * b1.sign;
            r.sign = a1.sign;
            q.trim(); r.trim();
            if(norm != 1) r /= norm;
            return std::make_pair(q, r);
        }

        void operator *= (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this * v;
        }

        BigInt operator / (const BigInt& v) const {
            return divmod(*this, v).first;
        }

        void operator /= (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this / v;
        }

        BigInt operator % (const BigInt& v) const {
            return divmod(*this, v).second;
        }

        void operator %= (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this % v;
        }

        #undef vll
        #pragma endregion
    };
};

void solve(){
    BigInt::BigInt a, b;
    std::cin >> a >> b;
    std::cout << (a - b).abs() << std::endl;
}

signed main(){
    std::cin.tie(0) -> std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}
