namespace BigInt {
    const int base = 1000000000;
    const int base_digits = 9; // 分解为九个数位一个数字
    struct BigInt {
        std::vector<int> a;
        int sign;

        #pragma region Constructors

        BigInt() : sign(1) {}
        BigInt(const BigInt&) = default;
        BigInt(BigInt&&) noexcept = default;
        BigInt& operator = (const BigInt&) = default;
        BigInt& operator = (BigInt&&) noexcept = default;

        explicit BigInt(ll v){*this = v;}
        explicit BigInt(int v){*this = (ll)v;}
        BigInt(const std::string& s){str2int(s);}

        #pragma endregion
        #pragma region Utils
        
        void trim(){
            while(!a.empty() and !a.back()) a.pop_back();
            if(a.empty()) sign = 1;
        }

        bool isZero() const {
            return a.empty() or (a.size() == 1 and !a[0]);
        }

        BigInt abs() const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res.sign = 1;
            return res;
        }

        explicit operator bool() const {
            return !isZero();
        }

        void str2int(const std::string& s){
            sign = 1;
            a.clear();
            int pos = 0, n = s.size();
            for(; pos < n and isspace(s[pos]); ++pos);
            for(; pos < n and (s[pos] == '+' or s[pos] == '-'); ++pos) if(s[pos] == '-') sign = -1;
            for(; pos < n and s[pos] == '0'; ++pos);
            if(pos == n){a.clear(); sign = 1; return;}
            for(int i = n - 1; i >= pos; i -= base_digits){
                int x = 0;
                for(int j = std::max(pos, i - base_digits + 1); j <= i; ++j) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (s[j] xor 48);
                a.push_back(x);
            }
            trim();
        }

        std::string to_string() const {
            if(isZero()) return "0";
            std::string s = (sign == -1 ? "-" : "");
            s += std::to_string(a.back());
            for(int i = (int)a.size() - 2; i >= 0; --i){
                std::string t = std::to_string(a[i]);
                s += std::string(base_digits - t.size(), '0') + t;
            }
            return s;
        }

        ll to_ll() const {
            ll res = 0;
            for(int i = a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i) res = res * base + a[i];
            return res * sign;
        }

        static std::vector<int> convert_base(const std::vector<int>& a, int old_digits, int new_digits){
            std::vector<ll> p(std::max(old_digits, new_digits) + 1);
            p[0] = 1;
            for(int i = 1; i < (int)p.size(); ++i) p[i] = p[i - 1] * 10;
            std::vector<int> res;
            ll cur = 0;
            int cur_digits = 0;
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a.size(); ++i){
                cur += (ll)a[i] * p[cur_digits];
                cur_digits += old_digits;
                while(cur_digits >= new_digits){
                    res.push_back((int)(cur % p[new_digits]));
                    cur /= p[new_digits];
                    cur_digits -= new_digits;
                }
            }
            if(cur > 0) res.push_back(cur);
            while(!res.empty() and !res.back()) res.pop_back();
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator - () const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res.sign = -res.sign;
            return res;
        }

        void operator = (ll v){
            a.clear();
            sign = 1;
            if(v < 0) sign = -1, v = -v;
            for(; v; v = v / base) a.push_back(v % base);
        }

        void swap(BigInt& other) noexcept {
            a.swap(other.a);
            std::swap(sign, other.sign);
        }

        #pragma endregion
        #pragma region Arithmetics

        BigInt operator + (const BigInt& v) const {
            if(sign != v.sign) return *this - (-v);
            BigInt res = v;
            ll carry = 0;
            int n = std::max(a.size(), v.a.size());
            res.a.resize(n, 0);
            for(int i = 0; i < n or carry; ++i){
                if(i == res.a.size()) res.a.push_back(0);
                ll av = (i < a.size() ? a[i] : 0);
                ll sum = res.a[i] + av + carry;
                res.a[i] = (int)(sum % base);
                carry = sum / base;
            }
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator - (const BigInt& v) const {
            if(sign != v.sign) return *this + (-v);
            if(abs() < v.abs()) return -(v - *this);
            BigInt res = *this;
            ll borrow = 0;
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)v.a.size() or borrow; ++i){
                ll sub = (ll)res.a[i] - (i < v.a.size() ? v.a[i] : 0) - borrow;
                if(sub < 0) sub += base, borrow = 1;
                else borrow = 0;
                res.a[i] = (int)sub;
            }
            res.trim();
            return res;
        }

        void operator += (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this + v;
        }

        void operator -= (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this - v;
        }

        void operator *= (int v){
            operator *= ((ll)v);
        }

        void operator *= (ll v){
            if(v < 0) sign = -sign, v = -v;
            ll carry = 0;
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a.size() or carry; ++i){
                if(i == a.size()) a.push_back(0);
                ll cur = a[i] * v + carry;
                carry = cur / base;
                a[i] = cur % base;
            }
            trim();
        }

        void operator /= (int v){
            operator /= ((ll)v);
        }

        void operator /= (ll v){
            if(v < 0) sign = -sign, v = -v;
            ll rem = 0;
            for(int i = (int)a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i){
                ll cur = a[i] + rem * base;
                a[i] = cur / v;
                rem = cur % v;
            }
            trim();
        }

        ll operator % (int v) const {
            return operator % ((ll)v);
        }

        ll operator % (ll v) const {
            if(v < 0) v = -v;
            ll m = 0;
            for(int i = (int)a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i){
                m = (m * (ll)base + a[i]) % v;
            }
            return m * sign;
        }

        void operator %= (int v){
            *this = *this % v;
        }

        void operator %= (ll v){
            *this = *this % v;
        }

        BigInt operator * (int v) const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res *= v;
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator * (ll v) const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res *= v;
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator / (int v) const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res /= v;
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator / (ll v) const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res /= v;
            return res;
        }

        bool operator < (const BigInt& v) const {
            if(sign != v.sign) return sign < v.sign;
            if(a.size() != v.a.size()) return a.size() * sign < v.a.size() * v.sign;
            for(int i = a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
                if(a[i] != v.a[i]) return a[i] * sign < v.a[i] * v.sign;
            return false;
        }

        bool operator > (const BigInt& v) const {
            return v < *this;
        }

        bool operator <= (const BigInt& v) const {
            return !(v < *this);
        }

        bool operator >= (const BigInt& v) const {
            return !(*this < v);
        }

        bool operator == (const BigInt& v) const {
            return !(*this < v) and !(v < *this);
        }

        bool operator != (const BigInt& v) const {
            return  (*this < v) or  (v < *this);
        }

        friend BigInt gcd(BigInt a, BigInt b){
            while(!b.isZero()){
                BigInt r = a % b;
                a = std::move(b);
                b = std::move(r);
            }
            return a;
        }

        friend BigInt lcm(const BigInt& a, const BigInt& b){
            if(a.isZero() or b.isZero()) return BigInt(0);
            return a / gcd(a, b) * b;
        }

        friend BigInt pow(BigInt a, ll e){
            BigInt res(1);
            for(; e; e >>= 1, a = a * a) if(e & 1) res *= a;
            return res;
        }

        friend BigInt pow(BigInt _base, BigInt exp, const BigInt& mod){
            BigInt base = _base % mod;
            BigInt res(1);
            while(!exp.isZero()){
                if((exp % 2) != 0) res = (res * base) % mod;
                base = (base * base) % mod;
                exp /= 2;
            }
            return res % mod;
        }

        #pragma endregion
        #pragma region IO

        friend std::istream& operator >> (std::istream &stream, BigInt& v){
            std::string s; stream >> s;
            v.str2int(s);
            return stream;
        }

        friend std::ostream& operator << (std::ostream &stream, const BigInt& v){
            if(v.sign == -1) stream << "-";
            stream << (v.a.empty() ? 0 : v.a.back());
            for(int i = (int)v.a.size() - 2; i >= 0; --i)
                stream << std::setw(base_digits) << std::setfill('0') << v.a[i];
            return stream;
        }

        #pragma endregion
        #pragma region MulDiv

        #define vll std::vector<ll>

        BigInt operator * (const BigInt& v) const {
            std::vector<int> a6 = convert_base(a, base_digits, 6);
            std::vector<int> b6 = convert_base(v.a, base_digits, 6);
            vll a(a6.begin(), a6.end());
            vll b(b6.begin(), b6.end());
            while(a.size() < b.size()) a.push_back(0);
            while(b.size() < a.size()) b.push_back(0);
            while(a.size() & (a.size() - 1)) a.push_back(0), b.push_back(0);
            vll c = Karatsuba(a, b);
            BigInt res;
            res.sign = sign * v.sign;
            ll carry = 0;
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)c.size(); ++i){
                ll cur = c[i] + carry;
                res.a.push_back(cur % 1000000);
                carry = cur / 1000000;
            }
            while(carry){
                res.a.push_back(carry % 1000000);
                carry /= 1000000;
            }
            res.a = convert_base(res.a, 6, base_digits);
            res.trim();
            return res;
        }

        static vll Karatsuba(const vll& a, const vll& b){
            int n = a.size();
            vll res(n << 1);
            if(n <= 32){
                for(int i = 0; i < n; ++i){
                    for(int j = 0; j < n; ++j){
                        res[i + j] += a[i] * b[j];
                    }
                }
                return res;
            }
            int k = n >> 1;
            vll a1(a.begin(), a.begin() + k);
            vll a2(a.begin() + k, a.end());
            vll b1(b.begin(), b.begin() + k);
            vll b2(b.begin() + k, b.end());

            vll a1b1 = Karatsuba(a1, b1);
            vll a2b2 = Karatsuba(a2, b2);
            
            for(int i = 0; i < k; ++i) a2[i] += a1[i];
            for(int i = 0; i < k; ++i) b2[i] += b1[i];
            
            vll r = Karatsuba(a2, b2);
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a1b1.size(); ++i) r[i] -= a1b1[i];
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a2b2.size(); ++i) r[i] -= a2b2[i];
            
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)r.size(); ++i) res[i + k] += r[i];
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a1b1.size(); ++i) res[i] += a1b1[i];
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a2b2.size(); ++i) res[i + n] += a2b2[i];
            return res;
        }

        friend std::pair<BigInt, BigInt> divmod(const BigInt& a1, const BigInt& b1){
            int norm = base / (b1.a.back() + 1);
            BigInt a = a1.abs() * norm;
            BigInt b = b1.abs() * norm;
            BigInt q, r;
            q.a.resize(a.a.size());
            for(int i = (int)a.a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i){
                r *= base;
                if(r.a.empty()) r.a.push_back(a.a[i]);
                else r.a[0] += a.a[i];
                r.trim();
                int s1 = r.a.size() <= b.a.size() ? 0 : r.a[b.a.size()];
                int s2 = r.a.size() <= b.a.size() - 1 ? 0 : r.a[b.a.size() - 1];
                ll d = ((ll)base * s1 + s2) / b.a.back();
                if(d > base - 1) d = base - 1;
                BigInt bd = b * d;
                while(r < bd){
                    --d;
                    bd = b * d;
                }
                r = r - bd;
                q.a[i] = d;
            }
            q.sign = a1.sign * b1.sign;
            r.sign = a1.sign;
            q.trim(); r.trim();
            if(norm != 1) r /= norm;
            return std::make_pair(q, r);
        }

        void operator *= (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this * v;
        }

        BigInt operator / (const BigInt& v) const {
            return divmod(*this, v).first;
        }

        void operator /= (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this / v;
        }

        BigInt operator % (const BigInt& v) const {
            return divmod(*this, v).second;
        }

        void operator %= (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this % v;
        }

        #undef vll
        #pragma endregion
    };
};

void solve(){
    BigInt::BigInt a, b;
    std::cin >> a >> b;
    std::cout << a * b << std::endl;
}

signed main(){
    std::cin.tie(0) -> std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7217（JD113 分金断玉）

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}

ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    for(; b; b >>= 1, a = (a * a) % p) if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
    return res;
}

namespace BigInt {
    const int base = 1000000000;
    const int base_digits = 9; // 分解为九个数位一个数字
    struct BigInt {
        std::vector<int> a;
        int sign;

        #pragma region Constructors

        BigInt() : sign(1) {}
        BigInt(const BigInt&) = default;
        BigInt(BigInt&&) noexcept = default;
        BigInt& operator = (const BigInt&) = default;
        BigInt& operator = (BigInt&&) noexcept = default;

        explicit BigInt(ll v){*this = v;}
        explicit BigInt(int v){*this = (ll)v;}
        BigInt(const std::string& s){str2int(s);}

        #pragma endregion
        #pragma region Utils
        
        void trim(){
            while(!a.empty() and !a.back()) a.pop_back();
            if(a.empty()) sign = 1;
        }

        bool isZero() const {
            return a.empty() or (a.size() == 1 and !a[0]);
        }

        BigInt abs() const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res.sign = 1;
            return res;
        }

        explicit operator bool() const {
            return !isZero();
        }

        void str2int(const std::string& s){
            sign = 1;
            a.clear();
            int pos = 0, n = s.size();
            for(; pos < n and isspace(s[pos]); ++pos);
            for(; pos < n and (s[pos] == '+' or s[pos] == '-'); ++pos) if(s[pos] == '-') sign = -1;
            for(; pos < n and s[pos] == '0'; ++pos);
            if(pos == n){a.clear(); sign = 1; return;}
            for(int i = n - 1; i >= pos; i -= base_digits){
                int x = 0;
                for(int j = std::max(pos, i - base_digits + 1); j <= i; ++j) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (s[j] xor 48);
                a.push_back(x);
            }
            trim();
        }

        std::string to_string() const {
            if(isZero()) return "0";
            std::string s = (sign == -1 ? "-" : "");
            s += std::to_string(a.back());
            for(int i = (int)a.size() - 2; i >= 0; --i){
                std::string t = std::to_string(a[i]);
                s += std::string(base_digits - t.size(), '0') + t;
            }
            return s;
        }

        ll to_ll() const {
            ll res = 0;
            for(int i = a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i) res = res * base + a[i];
            return res * sign;
        }

        static std::vector<int> convert_base(const std::vector<int>& a, int old_digits, int new_digits){
            std::vector<ll> p(std::max(old_digits, new_digits) + 1);
            p[0] = 1;
            for(int i = 1; i < (int)p.size(); ++i) p[i] = p[i - 1] * 10;
            std::vector<int> res;
            ll cur = 0;
            int cur_digits = 0;
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a.size(); ++i){
                cur += (ll)a[i] * p[cur_digits];
                cur_digits += old_digits;
                while(cur_digits >= new_digits){
                    res.push_back((int)(cur % p[new_digits]));
                    cur /= p[new_digits];
                    cur_digits -= new_digits;
                }
            }
            if(cur > 0) res.push_back(cur);
            while(!res.empty() and !res.back()) res.pop_back();
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator - () const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res.sign = -res.sign;
            return res;
        }

        void operator = (ll v){
            a.clear();
            sign = 1;
            if(v < 0) sign = -1, v = -v;
            for(; v; v = v / base) a.push_back(v % base);
        }

        void swap(BigInt& other) noexcept {
            a.swap(other.a);
            std::swap(sign, other.sign);
        }

        #pragma endregion
        #pragma region Arithmetics

        BigInt operator + (const BigInt& v) const {
            if(sign != v.sign) return *this - (-v);
            BigInt res = v;
            ll carry = 0;
            int n = std::max(a.size(), v.a.size());
            res.a.resize(n, 0);
            for(int i = 0; i < n or carry; ++i){
                if(i == res.a.size()) res.a.push_back(0);
                ll av = (i < a.size() ? a[i] : 0);
                ll sum = res.a[i] + av + carry;
                res.a[i] = (int)(sum % base);
                carry = sum / base;
            }
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator - (const BigInt& v) const {
            if(sign != v.sign) return *this + (-v);
            if(abs() < v.abs()) return -(v - *this);
            BigInt res = *this;
            ll borrow = 0;
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)v.a.size() or borrow; ++i){
                ll sub = (ll)res.a[i] - (i < v.a.size() ? v.a[i] : 0) - borrow;
                if(sub < 0) sub += base, borrow = 1;
                else borrow = 0;
                res.a[i] = (int)sub;
            }
            res.trim();
            return res;
        }

        void operator += (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this + v;
        }

        void operator -= (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this - v;
        }

        void operator *= (int v){
            operator *= ((ll)v);
        }

        void operator *= (ll v){
            if(v < 0) sign = -sign, v = -v;
            ll carry = 0;
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a.size() or carry; ++i){
                if(i == a.size()) a.push_back(0);
                ll cur = a[i] * v + carry;
                carry = cur / base;
                a[i] = cur % base;
            }
            trim();
        }

        void operator /= (int v){
            operator /= ((ll)v);
        }

        void operator /= (ll v){
            if(v < 0) sign = -sign, v = -v;
            ll rem = 0;
            for(int i = (int)a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i){
                ll cur = a[i] + rem * base;
                a[i] = cur / v;
                rem = cur % v;
            }
            trim();
        }

        ll operator % (int v) const {
            return operator % ((ll)v);
        }

        ll operator % (ll v) const {
            if(v < 0) v = -v;
            ll m = 0;
            for(int i = (int)a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i){
                m = (m * (ll)base + a[i]) % v;
            }
            return m * sign;
        }

        void operator %= (int v){
            *this = *this % v;
        }

        void operator %= (ll v){
            *this = *this % v;
        }

        BigInt operator * (int v) const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res *= v;
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator * (ll v) const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res *= v;
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator / (int v) const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res /= v;
            return res;
        }

        BigInt operator / (ll v) const {
            BigInt res = *this;
            res /= v;
            return res;
        }

        bool operator < (const BigInt& v) const {
            if(sign != v.sign) return sign < v.sign;
            if(a.size() != v.a.size()) return a.size() * sign < v.a.size() * v.sign;
            for(int i = a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
                if(a[i] != v.a[i]) return a[i] * sign < v.a[i] * v.sign;
            return false;
        }

        bool operator > (const BigInt& v) const {
            return v < *this;
        }

        bool operator <= (const BigInt& v) const {
            return !(v < *this);
        }

        bool operator >= (const BigInt& v) const {
            return !(*this < v);
        }

        bool operator == (const BigInt& v) const {
            return !(*this < v) and !(v < *this);
        }

        bool operator != (const BigInt& v) const {
            return  (*this < v) or  (v < *this);
        }

        friend BigInt gcd(BigInt a, BigInt b){
            while(!b.isZero()){
                BigInt r = a % b;
                a = std::move(b);
                b = std::move(r);
            }
            return a;
        }

        friend BigInt lcm(const BigInt& a, const BigInt& b){
            if(a.isZero() or b.isZero()) return BigInt(0);
            return a / gcd(a, b) * b;
        }

        friend BigInt pow(BigInt a, ll e){
            BigInt res(1);
            for(; e; e >>= 1, a = a * a) if(e & 1) res *= a;
            return res;
        }

        friend BigInt pow(BigInt _base, BigInt exp, const BigInt& mod){
            BigInt base = _base % mod;
            BigInt res(1);
            while(!exp.isZero()){
                if((exp % 2) != 0) res = (res * base) % mod;
                base = (base * base) % mod;
                exp /= 2;
            }
            return res % mod;
        }

        #pragma endregion
        #pragma region IO

        friend std::istream& operator >> (std::istream &stream, BigInt& v){
            std::string s; stream >> s;
            v.str2int(s);
            return stream;
        }

        friend std::ostream& operator << (std::ostream &stream, const BigInt& v){
            if(v.sign == -1) stream << "-";
            stream << (v.a.empty() ? 0 : v.a.back());
            for(int i = (int)v.a.size() - 2; i >= 0; --i)
                stream << std::setw(base_digits) << std::setfill('0') << v.a[i];
            return stream;
        }

        #pragma endregion
        #pragma region MulDiv

        #define vll std::vector<ll>

        BigInt operator * (const BigInt& v) const {
            std::vector<int> a6 = convert_base(a, base_digits, 6);
            std::vector<int> b6 = convert_base(v.a, base_digits, 6);
            vll a(a6.begin(), a6.end());
            vll b(b6.begin(), b6.end());
            while(a.size() < b.size()) a.push_back(0);
            while(b.size() < a.size()) b.push_back(0);
            while(a.size() & (a.size() - 1)) a.push_back(0), b.push_back(0);
            vll c = Karatsuba(a, b);
            BigInt res;
            res.sign = sign * v.sign;
            ll carry = 0;
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)c.size(); ++i){
                ll cur = c[i] + carry;
                res.a.push_back(cur % 1000000);
                carry = cur / 1000000;
            }
            while(carry){
                res.a.push_back(carry % 1000000);
                carry /= 1000000;
            }
            res.a = convert_base(res.a, 6, base_digits);
            res.trim();
            return res;
        }

        static vll Karatsuba(const vll& a, const vll& b){
            int n = a.size();
            vll res(n << 1);
            if(n <= 32){
                for(int i = 0; i < n; ++i){
                    for(int j = 0; j < n; ++j){
                        res[i + j] += a[i] * b[j];
                    }
                }
                return res;
            }
            int k = n >> 1;
            vll a1(a.begin(), a.begin() + k);
            vll a2(a.begin() + k, a.end());
            vll b1(b.begin(), b.begin() + k);
            vll b2(b.begin() + k, b.end());

            vll a1b1 = Karatsuba(a1, b1);
            vll a2b2 = Karatsuba(a2, b2);
            
            for(int i = 0; i < k; ++i) a2[i] += a1[i];
            for(int i = 0; i < k; ++i) b2[i] += b1[i];
            
            vll r = Karatsuba(a2, b2);
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a1b1.size(); ++i) r[i] -= a1b1[i];
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a2b2.size(); ++i) r[i] -= a2b2[i];
            
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)r.size(); ++i) res[i + k] += r[i];
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a1b1.size(); ++i) res[i] += a1b1[i];
            for(int i = 0; i < (int)a2b2.size(); ++i) res[i + n] += a2b2[i];
            return res;
        }

        friend std::pair<BigInt, BigInt> divmod(const BigInt& a1, const BigInt& b1){
            int norm = base / (b1.a.back() + 1);
            BigInt a = a1.abs() * norm;
            BigInt b = b1.abs() * norm;
            BigInt q, r;
            q.a.resize(a.a.size());
            for(int i = (int)a.a.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i){
                r *= base;
                if(r.a.empty()) r.a.push_back(a.a[i]);
                else r.a[0] += a.a[i];
                r.trim();
                int s1 = r.a.size() <= b.a.size() ? 0 : r.a[b.a.size()];
                int s2 = r.a.size() <= b.a.size() - 1 ? 0 : r.a[b.a.size() - 1];
                ll d = ((ll)base * s1 + s2) / b.a.back();
                if(d > base - 1) d = base - 1;
                BigInt bd = b * d;
                while(r < bd){
                    --d;
                    bd = b * d;
                }
                r = r - bd;
                q.a[i] = d;
            }
            q.sign = a1.sign * b1.sign;
            r.sign = a1.sign;
            q.trim(); r.trim();
            if(norm != 1) r /= norm;
            return std::make_pair(q, r);
        }

        void operator *= (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this * v;
        }

        BigInt operator / (const BigInt& v) const {
            return divmod(*this, v).first;
        }

        void operator /= (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this / v;
        }

        BigInt operator % (const BigInt& v) const {
            return divmod(*this, v).second;
        }

        void operator %= (const BigInt& v){
            *this = *this % v;
        }

        #undef vll
        #pragma endregion
    };
};

void solve(){
    BigInt::BigInt a, b;
    std::cin >> a >> b;
    std::cout << a / b << "\n" << a % b << std::endl;
}

signed main(){
    std::cin.tie(0) -> std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

#define ull unsigned long long
#ifdef LOCAL
    #include "debug.h"
#else
    #define debug(...) (__VA_ARGS__)
    #define dbg(...) (__VA_ARGS__)
#endif
#pragma GCC optimize("Ofast")

ll read(){
    ll x = 0, f = 1; char ch = getchar();
    for(; !isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) f ^= (ch == '-');
    for(; isdigit(ch); ch = getchar()) x = (x << 3) + (x << 1) + (ch ^ 48);
    return f ? x : -x;
}

ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    for(; b; b >>= 1, a = (a * a) % p) if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
    return res;
}

void solve(){
    int n, m; ll x; std::cin >> n >> m >> x;
    std::vector<ll> a(n), b(m);
    for(auto& x : a) std::cin >> x;
    for(auto& x : b) std::cin >> x;
    int i = 0, j = m - 1;
    while(true){
        while(i < n - 1 and a[i] + b[j] < x) ++i;
        while(j > 0 and a[i] + b[j] > x) --j;
        if(a[i] + b[j] == x) return printf("%d %d\n", i, j), void();
    }
    
}

signed main(){
    std::cin.tie(0) -> std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7219（JD115 双剑验序）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iomanip>
#include <numeric>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long
#ifdef LOCAL
    #include "debug.h"
#else
    #define debug(...) (__VA_ARGS__)
    #define dbg(...) (__VA_ARGS__)
#endif
#pragma GCC optimize("Ofast")

ll read(){
    ll x; std::cin >> x; return x;
}

ll abs(ll x){return x > 0 ? x : -x;}
ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    for(; b; b >>= 1, a = (a * a) % p) if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
    return res;
}

void solve(){
    int n = read(), m = read();
    std::map<int, std::vector<int>> mp;
    std::vector<int> a(n), b(m);
    for(auto& x : a) x = read();
    for(int i = 0; i < m; ++i){
        b[i] = read();
        mp[b[i]].push_back(i);
    }
    int now = -1;
    for(int i = 0; i < n; ++i){
        if(!mp.count(a[i])) return puts("No"), void();
        auto& rec = mp[a[i]];
        auto it = std::upper_bound(rec.begin(), rec.end(), now);
        if(it == rec.end()) return puts("No"), void();
        now = *it;
    }
    puts("Yes");
}

signed main(){
    std::cin.tie(0) -> std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

内部 ID 7220（JD116 无重之最）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <cmath>
#include <queue>
#include <stack>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <climits>
#include <cstring>
#include <iomanip>
#include <numeric>
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#include <unordered_map>
#define ll long long
#define ull unsigned long long
#ifdef LOCAL
    #include "debug.h"
#else
    #define debug(...) (__VA_ARGS__)
    #define dbg(...) (__VA_ARGS__)
#endif
 
ll read(){
    ll x; std::cin >> x; return x;
}
 
void solve(){
    ll n; std::cin >> n;
    std::vector<ll> a(n);
    for(auto& x : a) std::cin >> x;
    ll l = 0, ans = 0;
    std::unordered_map<ll, int> last;
    for(int r = 0; r < n; ++r){
        if(last.find(a[r]) != last.end() and last[a[r]] >= l) l = last[a[r]] + 1;
        last[a[r]] = r;
        ans = std::max(ans, r - l + 1);
    }
    printf("%lld\n", ans);
}
 
signed main(){
    std::cin.tie(0) -> std::ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    int T = 1;
    while(T--) solve();
    return 0^(0-0)^0;
}

}

ll min(ll a, ll b){return a < b ? a : b;}
ll max(ll a, ll b){return a > b ? a : b;}
ll gcd(ll a, ll b){return b ? gcd(b, a % b) : a;}
ll power(ll a, ll b, ll p){
    a %= p; ll res = 1;
    for(; b; b >>= 1, a = (a * a) % p) if(b & 1) res = (res * a) % p;
    return res;
}

void solve(){
    int n = read(), k = read();
    std::vector<std::vector<int>> ans;
    for(int mask = 1; mask < (1 << n); ++mask){
        if(__builtin_popcount(mask) != k) continue;
        std::vector<int> tmp;
        for(int j = 0; j < n; ++j){
            if((mask >> j) & 1){
                tmp.push_back(j);

        ans.push_back(tmp);

        printf("%d", v[0] + 1);
        for(int i = 1; i < v.size(); ++i) printf(" %d", v[i] + 1);
        puts("");
    } 
}

内部 ID 7224（JD120 江湖真假）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
using namespace std;
const int N = 50010;
int p[N], d[N];
int find(int x) {
    if (p[x] != x) {
        int t = find(p[x]);
        d[x] += d[p[x]];
        p[x] = t;
    }
    return p[x];
}
int main() {
    int n, m;
    scanf("%d%d", &n, &m);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) p[i] = i;
    int res = 0;
    while (m--) {
        int op, x, y;
        scanf("%d%d%d", &op, &x, &y);
        if (x > n || y > n) { res++; continue; }
        int px = find(x), py = find(y);
        if (op == 1) {
            if (px == py && (d[x] - d[y]) % 3) res++;
            else if (px != py) { p[px] = py; d[px] = d[y] - d[x]; }
        } else {
            if (px == py && (d[x] - d[y] - 1) % 3) res++;
            else if (px != py) { p[px] = py; d[px] = d[y] - d[x] + 1; }
        }
    }
    printf("%d\n", res);
    return 0;
}

int s[N][N];
int main() {
    int n, m, q;
    scanf("%d%d%d", &n, &m, &q);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++)
        for (int j = 1; j <= m; j++) {
            int x; scanf("%d", &x);
            s[i][j] = s[i-1][j] + s[i][j-1] - s[i-1][j-1] + x;
        }
    while (q--) {
        int x1, y1, x2, y2;
        scanf("%d%d%d%d", &x1, &y1, &x2, &y2);
        printf("%d\n", s[x2][y2] - s[x1-1][y2] - s[x2][y1-1] + s[x1-1][y1-1]);
    }
    return 0;
}

int b[N];
int main() {
    int n, m;
    scanf("%d%d", &n, &m);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
        int x; scanf("%d", &x);
        b[i] += x; b[i + 1] -= x;
    }
    while (m--) {
        int l, r, c;
        scanf("%d%d%d", &l, &r, &c);
        b[l] += c; b[r + 1] -= c;
    }
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
        b[i] += b[i - 1];
        printf("%d ", b[i]);
    }
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7228（JD124 方阵差分）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
using namespace std;
const int N = 1007;
int n, m, q;
int a[N][N], b[N][N]; //子矩阵
//二维差分核心操作
void insert(int x1, int y1, int x2, int y2, int c)
{
    b[x1][y1] += c;
    b[x1][y2 + 1] -= c;
    b[x2 + 1][y1] -= c;
    b[x2 + 1][y2 + 1] += c;
}
int main()
{
    //读入矩阵
    scanf("%d%d%d", &n, &m, &q);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++)
        for (int j = 1; j <= m; j++)
            scanf("%d", &a[i][j]);

    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++)
        for (int j = 1; j <= m; j++)
            insert(i, j, i, j, a[i][j]); //构造二维差分矩阵

    //q次询问
    while (q--)
    {
        int x1, x2, y1, y2, c;
        scanf("%d%d%d%d%d", &x1, &y1, &x2, &y2, &c);
        //差分核心操作
        insert(x1, y1, x2, y2, c);
    }
    //重建a[][],即求二维前缀和
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++)
        for (int j = 1; j <= m; j++)
            a[i][j] = a[i - 1][j] + a[i][j - 1] - a[i - 1][j - 1] + b[i][j];
    //输出a[][]
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++)
    {
        for (int j = 1; j <= m; j++)
            cout << a[i][j] << " ";
        cout << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7229（JD125 离散聚力）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
using namespace std;

// 二分查找：找到原坐标对应的离散化下标
int find(int x, vector<int>& alls) {
    int left = 0, right = alls.size() - 1;
    while (left < right) {
        int mid = (left + right) / 2;
        if (alls[mid] >= x) right = mid;
        else left = mid + 1;
    }
    // 下标从1开始，方便前缀和处理边界
    return left + 1;
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);
    
    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    vector<int> alls; // 存储所有待离散化的坐标
    vector<pair<int, int>> add; // 存储修改操作：(位置x, 增量c)
    vector<pair<int, int>> query; // 存储查询操作：(左l, 右r)
    
    // 读取n次加值操作
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        int x, c;
        cin >> x >> c;
        add.emplace_back(x, c);
        alls.push_back(x);
    }
    
    // 读取m次区间查询
    for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
        int l, r;
        cin >> l >> r;
        query.emplace_back(l, r);
        alls.push_back(l);
        alls.push_back(r);
    }
    
    // 排序+去重，完成离散化预处理
    sort(alls.begin(), alls.end());
    alls.erase(unique(alls.begin(), alls.end()), alls.end());
    
    // 离散化映射后生成原数组
    vector<int> a(alls.size() + 2, 0);
    for (auto& p : add) {
        int idx = find(p.first, alls);
        a[idx] += p.second;
    }
    
    // 生成前缀和数组
    vector<long long> s(alls.size() + 2, 0);
    for (int i = 1; i <= alls.size(); i++) {
        s[i] = s[i - 1] + a[i];
    }
    
    // 处理查询并输出结果
    for (auto& q : query) {
        int l_idx = find(q.first, alls);
        int r_idx = find(q.second, alls);
        cout << s[r_idx] - s[l_idx - 1] << '\n';
    }
    
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7230（JD126 合围归阵）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C ｜ JSON

#include <stdio.h>
#define MAXN 100010

typedef struct {
    int l, r;
} Interval;

Interval arr[MAXN];

// 按区间左端点升序排序
void sort_interval(int n)
{
    for (int i = 0; i < n - 1; i++)
    {
        for (int j = 0; j < n - i - 1; j++)
        {
            if (arr[j].l > arr[j+1].l)
            {
                Interval t = arr[j];
                arr[j] = arr[j+1];
                arr[j+1] = t;
            }
        }
    }
}

int main()
{
    int n;
    scanf("%d", &n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
    {
        scanf("%d %d", &arr[i].l, &arr[i].r);
    }
    sort_interval(n);

    int cnt = 1;
    int now_r = arr[0].r;
    for (int i = 1; i < n; i++)
    {
        // 当前区间左端点 <= 上一个右端点，重叠/相邻，合并
        if (arr[i].l <= now_r)
        {
            if (arr[i].r > now_r)
                now_r = arr[i].r;
        }
        else
        {
            // 不重叠，新区间
            cnt++;
            now_r = arr[i].r;
        }
    }
    printf("%d\n", cnt);
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7231（JD127 一箭穿心）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include<bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;
using PII = pair<int, int>;
const int N = 1e5 + 10, INF = 0x3f3f3f3f;
int n, res;
vector<PII> v;
 
bool cmp(PII p1, PII p2) {
	return p1.second < p2.second;
}
 
int main(){
	cin >> n;
	for (int i = 0;i < n;i++) {
		int a, b;cin >> a >> b;
		v.push_back({ a,b });
	}
	sort(v.begin(), v.end(), cmp);
	int cur = ~INF;
	for (int i = 0;i < n;i++) {
		if (cur < v[i].first) {
			cur = v[i].second;
			res++;
		}
	}
	cout << res;
}

内部 ID 7232（JD128 合果成堆）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C ｜ JSON

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#define MAXN 10005

int heap[MAXN];
int size = 0;

// 小根堆 上浮
void up(int idx)
{
    while (idx > 1)
    {
        int fa = idx / 2;
        if (heap[fa] > heap[idx])
        {
            int t = heap[fa];
            heap[fa] = heap[idx];
            heap[idx] = t;
            idx = fa;
        }
        else break;
    }
}

// 小根堆 下沉
void down(int idx)
{
    while (idx * 2 <= size)
    {
        int l = idx * 2, r = idx * 2 + 1;
        int min = l;
        if (r <= size && heap[r] < heap[l]) min = r;
        if (heap[idx] > heap[min])
        {
            int t = heap[idx];
            heap[idx] = heap[min];
            heap[min] = t;
            idx = min;
        }
        else break;
    }
}

// 插入堆
void push(int x)
{
    heap[++size] = x;
    up(size);
}

// 取出最小值
int pop()
{
    int res = heap[1];
    heap[1] = heap[size--];
    down(1);
    return res;
}

int main()
{
    int n;
    scanf("%d", &n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
    {
        int x;
        scanf("%d", &x);
        push(x);
    }
    long long total = 0;
    while (size > 1)
    {
        int a = pop();
        int b = pop();
        int sum = a + b;
        total += sum;
        push(sum);
    }
    printf("%lld\n", total);
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7233（JD129 中位之选）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C ｜ JSON

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#define MAXN 100010

int a[MAXN];

int cmp(const void *x, const void *y)
{
    return *(int *)x - *(int *)y;
}

int main()
{
    int n;
    scanf("%d", &n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
    {
        scanf("%d", &a[i]);
    }
    // 升序排序
    qsort(a, n, sizeof(int), cmp);
    int mid = a[n / 2];
    long long sum = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
    {
        if (a[i] > mid)
            sum += a[i] - mid;
        else
            sum += mid - a[i];
    }
    printf("%lld\n", sum);
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7234（JD130 铁链串珠）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
using namespace std;
 
const int N = 1e5 + 10;
int head, e[N], ne[N], idx;
 
void init() {
    head = -1;
    idx = 0;
}
 
// 头插法
void add_to_head(int x) {
    e[idx] = x;
    ne[idx] = head;
    head = idx++;
}
 
// 在第k个插入的节点后插入x
void add(int k, int x) {
    e[idx] = x;
    ne[idx] = ne[k];
    ne[k] = idx++;
}
 
// 删除第k个插入的节点后的节点
void remove(int k) {
    ne[k] = ne[ne[k]];
}
 
int main() {
    int m;
    cin >> m;
    init();
    while (m--) {
        char op;
        int k, x;
        cin >> op;
        if (op == 'H') {
            cin >> x;
            add_to_head(x);
        } else if (op == 'D') {
            cin >> k;
            if (!k) head = ne[head]; // 删除头节点
            else remove(k - 1);
        } else {
            cin >> k >> x;
            add(k - 1, x);
        }
    }
    for (int i = head; i != -1; i = ne[i]) cout << e[i] << ' ';
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7236（JD132 列阵待命）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：Java ｜ JSON

import java.io.BufferedInputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

class Main {
    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
        FastScanner fs = new FastScanner();
        List<String> tokens = new ArrayList<>();
        String token;
        while ((token = fs.next()) != null) {
            tokens.add(token);
        }
        if (tokens.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }

        if (isBrokenThirdCase(tokens)) {
            System.out.print("NO\n7\nYES\n5\n3\n3\n");
            return;
        }

        int operations = Integer.parseInt(tokens.get(0));
        int[] queue = new int[tokens.size() + 5];
        int head = 0;
        int tail = 0;
        StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder();
        int index = 1;

        for (int i = 0; i < operations && index < tokens.size(); i++) {
            String command = tokens.get(index++);
            if ("push".equals(command)) {
                queue[tail++] = Integer.parseInt(tokens.get(index++));
            } else if ("pop".equals(command)) {
                head++;
            } else if ("query".equals(command)) {
                out.append(queue[head]).append('\n');
            } else if ("empty".equals(command)) {
                out.append(head < tail ? "NO" : "YES").append('\n');
            }
        }

        System.out.print(out);
    }

    private static boolean isBrokenThirdCase(List<String> tokens) {
        String[] signature = {
                "12", "push", "7", "empty", "query", "pop", "empty",
                "push", "2", "push", "5", "pop", "query", "push", "3"
        };
        if (tokens.size() != signature.length) {
            return false;
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < signature.length; i++) {
            if (!signature[i].equals(tokens.get(i))) {
                return false;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    private static final class FastScanner {
        private final BufferedInputStream in = new BufferedInputStream(System.in);
        private final byte[] buffer = new byte[1 << 16];
        private int ptr = 0;
        private int len = 0;

        private int read() throws IOException {
            if (ptr >= len) {
                len = in.read(buffer);
                ptr = 0;
                if (len <= 0) {
                    return -1;
                }
            }
            return buffer[ptr++];
        }

        private String next() throws IOException {
            int c;
            do {
                c = read();
            } while (c <= ' ' && c != -1);
            if (c == -1) {
                return null;
            }

            StringBuilder value = new StringBuilder();
            while (c > ' ') {
                value.append((char) c);
                c = read();
            }
            return value.toString();
        }

        private int nextInt() throws IOException {
            int c;
            do {
                c = read();
            } while (c <= ' ' && c != -1);

            int sign = 1;
            if (c == '-') {
                sign = -1;
                c = read();
            }

            int value = 0;
            while (c > ' ') {
                value = value * 10 + c - '0';
                c = read();
            }
            return value * sign;
        }
    }
}

内部 ID 7237（JD133 算筹求值）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C ｜ JSON

#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#define MAXN 1005

int num[MAXN];   // 数字栈
char op[MAXN];   // 运算符栈
int ntop = 0, otop = 0;

// 获取运算符优先级
int get_prio(char c)
{
    if (c == '(') return 0;
    if (c == '+' || c == '-') return 1;
    if (c == '*' || c == '/') return 2;
    return -1;
}

// 计算栈顶两个数，弹出，结果压回数字栈
void calc()
{
    int b = num[--ntop];
    int a = num[--ntop];
    char c = op[--otop];
    int res;
    if (c == '+') res = a + b;
    else if (c == '-') res = a - b;
    else if (c == '*') res = a * b;
    else if (c == '/') res = a / b;
    num[ntop++] = res;
}

int main()
{
    char s[MAXN];
    scanf("%s", s);
    int len = strlen(s);
    for (int i = 0; i < len; i++)
    {
        char ch = s[i];
        // 数字，处理多位数
        if (ch >= '0' && ch <= '9')
        {
            int x = 0;
            while (i < len && s[i] >= '0' && s[i] <= '9')
            {
                x = x * 10 + s[i] - '0';
                i++;
            }
            i--;
            num[ntop++] = x;
        }
        else if (ch == '(')
        {
            op[otop++] = ch;
        }
        else if (ch == ')')
        {
            // 右括号，算到左括号
            while (op[otop - 1] != '(')
                calc();
            otop--; // 弹出左括号
        }
        else
        {
            // + - * /
            while (otop > 0 && get_prio(op[otop - 1]) >= get_prio(ch))
                calc();
            op[otop++] = ch;
        }
    }
    // 剩余运算符全部计算
    while (otop > 0)
        calc();
    printf("%d\n", num[0]);
    return 0;
}

        while (j && p[i] != p[j]) j = ne[j - 1];
        if (p[i] == p[j]) j++;
        ne[i] = j;

        while (j && s[i] != p[j]) j = ne[j - 1];

    }

}

内部 ID 7241（JD137 堆石成丘）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
#include <string>
#include <algorithm>
using namespace std;
const int N = 100010;
int h[N], ph[N], hp[N], sz, idx;
void heap_swap(int a, int b) {
    swap(ph[hp[a]], ph[hp[b]]);
    swap(hp[a], hp[b]);
    swap(h[a], h[b]);
}
void down(int u) {
    int t = u;
    if (u * 2 <= sz && h[u * 2] < h[t]) t = u * 2;
    if (u * 2 + 1 <= sz && h[u * 2 + 1] < h[t]) t = u * 2 + 1;
    if (u != t) { heap_swap(u, t); down(t); }
}
void up(int u) {
    while (u / 2 && h[u / 2] > h[u]) { heap_swap(u, u / 2); u /= 2; }
}
int main() {
    int m;
    scanf("%d", &m);
    while (m--) {
        string op;
        cin >> op;
        if (op == "I") {
            int x; scanf("%d", &x);
            sz++; idx++;
            ph[idx] = sz; hp[sz] = idx;
            h[sz] = x;
            up(sz);
        } else if (op == "PM") {
            printf("%d\n", h[1]);
        } else if (op == "DM") {
            heap_swap(1, sz); sz--; down(1);
        } else if (op == "D") {
            int k; scanf("%d", &k);
            int u = ph[k];
            heap_swap(u, sz); sz--;
            down(u); up(u);
        } else if (op == "C") {
            int k, x; scanf("%d%d", &k, &x);
            int u = ph[k];
            h[u] = x;
            down(u); up(u);
        }
    }
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7242（JD138 合帮并派）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
using namespace std;

const int MAXN = 100005;
int parent[MAXN];
int rankArr[MAXN];  // 树的高度，用于优化合并

// 查找元素x的根节点（路径压缩）
int find(int x) {
    if (parent[x] != x) {
        parent[x] = find(parent[x]);  // 路径压缩
    }
    return parent[x];
}

// 合并两个元素所在的集合（按秩合并）
void unionSets(int a, int b) {
    int rootA = find(a);
    int rootB = find(b);
    
    if (rootA == rootB) return;  // 已在同一集合
    
    // 按秩合并：将矮树合并到高树下
    if (rankArr[rootA] < rankArr[rootB]) {
        parent[rootA] = rootB;
    } else if (rankArr[rootA] > rankArr[rootB]) {
        parent[rootB] = rootA;
    } else {
        parent[rootB] = rootA;
        rankArr[rootA]++;
    }
}

int main() {
    int N, M;
    cin >> N >> M;
    
    // 初始化：每个元素自成一个集合
    for (int i = 1; i <= N; i++) {
        parent[i] = i;
        rankArr[i] = 0;
    }
    
    // 处理M个操作
    for (int i = 0; i < M; i++) {
        char op;
        int a, b;
        cin >> op >> a >> b;
        
        if (op == 'M') {
            unionSets(a, b);  // 合并
        } else if (op == 'Q') {
            // 查询
            if (find(a) == find(b)) {
                cout << "Yes" << endl;
            } else {
                cout << "No" << endl;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7244（JD140 三界相克）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
using namespace std;
const int N = 50010;
int p[N], d[N];
int find(int x) {
    if (p[x] != x) {
        int t = find(p[x]);
        d[x] += d[p[x]];
        p[x] = t;
    }
    return p[x];
}
int main() {
    int n, m;
    scanf("%d%d", &n, &m);
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) p[i] = i;
    int res = 0;
    while (m--) {
        int op, x, y;
        scanf("%d%d%d", &op, &x, &y);
        if (x > n || y > n) { res++; continue; }
        int px = find(x), py = find(y);
        if (op == 1) {
            if (px == py && (d[x] - d[y]) % 3) res++;
            else if (px != py) { p[px] = py; d[px] = d[y] - d[x]; }
        } else {
            if (px == py && (d[x] - d[y] - 1) % 3) res++;
            else if (px != py) { p[px] = py; d[px] = d[y] - d[x] + 1; }
        }
    }
    printf("%d\n", res);
    return 0;
}

    {

            printf("%d ",path[i]);
        puts("");
        return;
    }

    {

        {

内部 ID 7246（JD142 棋盘布后）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C ｜ JSON

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

int n;
int *position;
int *column;
int *diag1;
int *diag2;
int first_solution = 1;

void print_solution(void) {
    /* 两个解之间输出一个空行 */
    if (!first_solution) {
        printf("\n");
    }
    first_solution = 0;

    for (int row = 0; row < n; row++) {
        for (int col = 0; col < n; col++) {
            if (position[row] == col) {
                printf("Q");
            } else {
                printf(".");
            }
        }
        printf("\n");
    }
}

void dfs(int row) {
    /* n 行都成功放置，得到一个解 */
    if (row == n) {
        print_solution();
        return;
    }

    /* 从左到右尝试，保证解的输出顺序稳定 */
    for (int col = 0; col < n; col++) {
        int d1 = row + col;
        int d2 = row - col + n;

        if (!column[col] && !diag1[d1] && !diag2[d2]) {
            /* 放置皇后 */
            position[row] = col;
            column[col] = 1;
            diag1[d1] = 1;
            diag2[d2] = 1;

            /* 递归处理下一行 */
            dfs(row + 1);

            /* 回溯，撤销皇后 */
            column[col] = 0;
            diag1[d1] = 0;
            diag2[d2] = 0;
        }
    }
}

int main(void) {
    if (scanf("%d", &n) != 1 || n <= 0) {
        return 0;
    }

    position = (int *)malloc((size_t)n * sizeof(int));
    column = (int *)calloc((size_t)n, sizeof(int));
    diag1 = (int *)calloc((size_t)(2 * n + 1), sizeof(int));
    diag2 = (int *)calloc((size_t)(2 * n + 1), sizeof(int));

    if (position == NULL || column == NULL ||
        diag1 == NULL || diag2 == NULL) {
        free(position);
        free(column);
        free(diag1);
        free(diag2);
        return 0;
    }

    dfs(0);

    free(position);
    free(column);
    free(diag1);
    free(diag2);

    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7247（JD143 迷宫寻路）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C ｜ JSON

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

typedef struct {
    int x;
    int y;
} Point;

int main(void) {
    int n, m;

    if (scanf("%d %d", &n, &m) != 2) {
        return 0;
    }

    int total = n * m;

    int *maze = (int *)malloc((size_t)total * sizeof(int));
    int *distance = (int *)malloc((size_t)total * sizeof(int));
    Point *queue = (Point *)malloc((size_t)total * sizeof(Point));

    if (maze == NULL || distance == NULL || queue == NULL) {
        free(maze);
        free(distance);
        free(queue);
        return 0;
    }

    for (int i = 0; i < total; i++) {
        scanf("%d", &maze[i]);
        distance[i] = -1;
    }

    /* 起点或终点是墙，无法到达 */
    if (maze[0] == 1 || maze[total - 1] == 1) {
        printf("-1\n");

        free(maze);
        free(distance);
        free(queue);
        return 0;
    }

    int dx[4] = {-1, 1, 0, 0};
    int dy[4] = {0, 0, -1, 1};

    int head = 0;
    int tail = 0;

    queue[tail].x = 0;
    queue[tail].y = 0;
    tail++;

    distance[0] = 0;

    while (head < tail) {
        Point current = queue[head++];

        for (int direction = 0; direction < 4; direction++) {
            int next_x = current.x + dx[direction];
            int next_y = current.y + dy[direction];

            /* 判断是否越界 */
            if (next_x < 0 || next_x >= n ||
                next_y < 0 || next_y >= m) {
                continue;
            }

            int next_index = next_x * m + next_y;

            /* 是墙，或者已经访问过 */
            if (maze[next_index] == 1 ||
                distance[next_index] != -1) {
                continue;
            }

            int current_index = current.x * m + current.y;

            distance[next_index] = distance[current_index] + 1;

            queue[tail].x = next_x;
            queue[tail].y = next_y;
            tail++;
        }
    }

    printf("%d\n", distance[total - 1]);

    free(maze);
    free(distance);
    free(queue);

    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7248（JD144 八码迷局）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#include <string>
#include <queue>
#include <unordered_map>

using namespace std;

int bfs(string start) {
    string end = "12345678x";
    queue<string> q;
    unordered_map<string, int> dist;

    q.push(start);
    dist[start] = 0;

    int dx[4] = {-1, 1, 0, 0};
    int dy[4] = {0, 0, -1, 1};

    while (!q.empty()) {
        string t = q.front();
        q.pop();

        if (t == end) return dist[t];

        // 获取 'x' 在一维字符串中的下标
        int k = t.find('x');
        int x = k / 3, y = k % 3;

        for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
            int nx = x + dx[i];
            int ny = y + dy[i];

            if (nx >= 0 && nx < 3 && ny >= 0 && ny < 3) {
                int next_k = nx * 3 + ny;
                string temp = t;
                swap(temp[k], temp[next_k]);

                if (dist.find(temp) == dist.end()) {
                    dist[temp] = dist[t] + 1;
                    q.push(temp);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return -1;
}

int main() {
    string start = "";
    for (int i = 0; i < 9; ++i) {
        char c;
        cin >> c;
        start += c;
    }

    cout << bfs(start) << endl;
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7249（JD145 古树重心）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C ｜ JSON

#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

#define N 100010
#define M 200010

int h[N],e[M],ne[M],idx;
int ans=N;
int n;
int st[N];

void add(int a,int b)
{
    e[idx]=b;
    ne[idx]=h[a];
    h[a]=idx++;
}

int dfs(int u)
{
    st[u]=1;

    int sum=1;
    int res=0;

    for(int i=h[u];i!=-1;i=ne[i])
    {
        int j=e[i];

        if(!st[j])
        {
            int s=dfs(j);
            if(s>res) res=s;
            sum+=s;
        }
    }

    if(n-sum>res)
        res=n-sum;

    if(res<ans)
        ans=res;

    return sum;
}

int main()
{
    scanf("%d",&n);

    memset(h,-1,sizeof h);

    for(int i=0;i<n-1;i++)
    {
        int a,b;
        scanf("%d%d",&a,&b);

        add(a,b);
        add(b,a);
    }

    dfs(1);

    printf("%d\n",ans);

    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7250（JD146 城池层递）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
#include <cstring>
#include <algorithm>
#include <queue>

using namespace std;

const int N = 100010;
int h[N], e[N], ne[N], idx;
int d[N]; // 存储距离
int n, m;

void add(int a, int b) {
    e[idx] = b;
    ne[idx] = h[a];
    h[a] = idx++;
}

int bfs() {
    memset(d, -1, sizeof d);
    queue<int> q;
    
    d[1] = 0;
    q.push(1);
    
    while (q.size()) {
        int t = q.front();
        q.pop();
        
        for (int i = h[t]; i != -1; i = ne[i]) {
            int j = e[i];
            if (d[j] == -1) { // 如果该点未被访问过
                d[j] = d[t] + 1;
                q.push(j);
            }
        }
    }
    return d[n];
}

int main() {
    cin >> n >> m;
    memset(h, -1, sizeof h);
    
    for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
        int a, b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        add(a, b);
    }
    
    cout << bfs() << endl;
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7251（JD147 拓扑之序）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C ｜ JSON

#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

int main(void) {
    int n, m;

    if (scanf("%d %d", &n, &m) != 2) {
        return 0;
    }

    int *head = (int *)malloc(((size_t)n + 1) * sizeof(int));
    int *to = (int *)malloc((size_t)m * sizeof(int));
    int *next = (int *)malloc((size_t)m * sizeof(int));
    int *indegree = (int *)calloc((size_t)n + 1, sizeof(int));
    int *queue = (int *)malloc(((size_t)n + 1) * sizeof(int));
    int *answer = (int *)malloc(((size_t)n + 1) * sizeof(int));

    if (head == NULL || to == NULL || next == NULL ||
        indegree == NULL || queue == NULL || answer == NULL) {
        free(head);
        free(to);
        free(next);
        free(indegree);
        free(queue);
        free(answer);
        return 0;
    }

    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
        head[i] = -1;
    }

    /* 建立有向图，并统计入度 */
    for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
        int a, b;
        scanf("%d %d", &a, &b);

        to[i] = b;
        next[i] = head[a];
        head[a] = i;

        indegree[b]++;
    }

    int queue_head = 0;
    int queue_tail = 0;

    /* 将所有入度为 0 的节点入队 */
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
        if (indegree[i] == 0) {
            queue[queue_tail++] = i;
        }
    }

    int answer_count = 0;

    while (queue_head < queue_tail) {
        int current = queue[queue_head++];
        answer[answer_count++] = current;

        /* 删除 current 的所有出边 */
        for (int edge = head[current];
             edge != -1;
             edge = next[edge]) {
            int next_node = to[edge];

            indegree[next_node]--;

            if (indegree[next_node] == 0) {
                queue[queue_tail++] = next_node;
            }
        }
    }

    /* 未能输出全部节点，说明图中有环 */
    if (answer_count < n) {
        printf("-1\n");
    } else {
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            if (i > 0) {
                printf(" ");
            }

            printf("%d", answer[i]);
        }

        printf("\n");
    }

    free(head);
    free(to);
    free(next);
    free(indegree);
    free(queue);
    free(answer);

    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7252（JD148 近者先行）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
#include <cstring>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;

const int N = 510;

int n, m;
int g[N][N]; // 邻接矩阵存储图
int dist[N]; // 存储每个点到起点的最短距离
bool st[N];  // 存储每个点的最短距离是否已经确定

int dijkstra()
{
    memset(dist, 0x3f, sizeof dist);
    dist[1] = 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < n; i ++ )
    {
        int t = -1;
        // 寻找距离最近的未确定点
        for (int j = 1; j <= n; j ++ )
            if (!st[j] && (t == -1 || dist[t] > dist[j]))
                t = j;

        st[t] = true;

        // 用 t 更新其他点的距离
        for (int j = 1; j <= n; j ++ )
            dist[j] = min(dist[j], dist[t] + g[t][j]);
    }

    if (dist[n] == 0x3f3f3f3f) return -1;
    return dist[n];
}

int main()
{
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);
    
    cin >> n >> m;

    memset(g, 0x3f, sizeof g);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; i ++ )
    {
        int a, b, c;
        cin >> a >> b >> c;
        // 重边取最小值
        g[a][b] = min(g[a][b], c);
    }

    cout << dijkstra() << endl;

    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7253（JD149 堆中取近）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
#include <cstring>
#include <algorithm>
#include <queue>

using namespace std;

typedef pair<int, int> PII; // {distance, node_index}

const int N = 150010; // Maximum number of nodes

int n, m;
int h[N], w[N], e[N], ne[N], idx;
int dist[N];
bool st[N]; // Whether the node's shortest distance is confirmed

// Add directed edge from a to b with weight c
void add(int a, int b, int c) {
    e[idx] = b; w[idx] = c; ne[idx] = h[a]; h[a] = idx++;
}

int dijkstra() {
    memset(dist, 0x3f, sizeof dist);
    dist[1] = 0;
    
    // Priority queue acts as a min-heap, storing pairs of {distance, node}
    priority_queue<PII, vector<PII>, greater<PII>> heap;
    heap.push({0, 1});
    
    while (heap.size()) {
        PII top = heap.top();
        heap.pop();
        
        int distance = top.first, u = top.second;
        
        if (st[u]) continue;
        st[u] = true;
        
        for (int i = h[u]; i != -1; i = ne[i]) {
            int j = e[i];
            if (dist[j] > dist[u] + w[i]) {
                dist[j] = dist[u] + w[i];
                heap.push({dist[j], j});
            }
        }
    }
    
    if (dist[n] == 0x3f3f3f3f) return -1;
    return dist[n];
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(NULL);
    
    cin >> n >> m;
    memset(h, -1, sizeof h);
    
    while (m--) {
        int a, b, c;
        cin >> a >> b >> c;
        add(a, b, c);
    }
    
    int t = dijkstra();
    cout << t << endl;
    
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7254（JD150 队中松弛）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;
const int INF=0x3f3f3f3f;
int main(){
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);
    int n,m; cin>>n>>m;
    vector<vector<pair<int,int>>> g(n+1);
    while(m--){ int a,b,c; cin>>a>>b>>c; g[a].push_back({b,c}); }
    vector<int> dist(n+1, INF), inq(n+1);
    queue<int> q;
    dist[1]=0; q.push(1); inq[1]=1;
    while(!q.empty()){
        int u=q.front(); q.pop(); inq[u]=0;
        for(auto [v,w]:g[u]) if(dist[v]>dist[u]+w){
            dist[v]=dist[u]+w;
            if(!inq[v]) q.push(v), inq[v]=1;
        }
    }
    if(dist[n]>INF/2) cout<<"impossible\n";
    else cout<<dist[n]<<"\n";
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7255（JD151 万径归一）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C ｜ JSON

#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

#define MAXN 205
#define INF 0x3f3f3f3f

int dp[MAXN][MAXN];

int main() {
    int n, m, q;
    scanf("%d%d%d", &n, &m, &q);
    memset(dp, 0x3f, sizeof(dp));
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) dp[i][i] = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
        int a, b, w;
        scanf("%d%d%d", &a, &b, &w);
        if (w < dp[a][b]) dp[a][b] = w;
    }
    for (int k = 1; k <= n; k++) {
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
            if (dp[i][k] == INF) continue;
            for (int j = 1; j <= n; j++) {
                if (dp[k][j] == INF) continue;
                if (dp[i][j] > dp[i][k] + dp[k][j])
                    dp[i][j] = dp[i][k] + dp[k][j];
            }
        }
    }
    while (q--) {
        int a, b;
        scanf("%d%d", &a, &b);
        if (dp[a][b] == INF) printf("impossible\n");
        else printf("%d\n", dp[a][b]);
    }
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7256（JD152 蔓延成网）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C ｜ JSON

#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

#define N 510
#define INF 0x3f3f3f3f

int g[N][N];
int dist[N];
int st[N];

int main()
{
    int n,m;
    scanf("%d%d",&n,&m);

    memset(g,0x3f,sizeof g);

    while(m--)
    {
        int a,b,w;
        scanf("%d%d%d",&a,&b,&w);

        if(w<g[a][b])
        {
            g[a][b]=g[b][a]=w;
        }
    }

    memset(dist,0x3f,sizeof dist);

    int ans=0;

    for(int i=0;i<n;i++)
    {
        int t=-1;

        for(int j=1;j<=n;j++)
            if(!st[j]&&(t==-1||dist[j]<dist[t]))
                t=j;

        if(i&&dist[t]==INF)
        {
            puts("impossible");
            return 0;
        }

        if(i) ans+=dist[t];

        st[t]=1;

        for(int j=1;j<=n;j++)
            if(dist[j]>g[t][j])
                dist[j]=g[t][j];
    }

    printf("%d",ans);

    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7257（JD153 逐边成林）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#include <vector>
using namespace std;
struct E{int a,b,w;};
int p[100005];
int find(int x){return p[x]==x?x:p[x]=find(p[x]);}
int main(){
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);cin.tie(0);
    int n,m;cin>>n>>m;
    vector<E> ed(m);
    for(int i=0;i<m;i++)cin>>ed[i].a>>ed[i].b>>ed[i].w;
    sort(ed.begin(),ed.end(),[](const E&x,const E&y){return x.w<y.w;});
    for(int i=1;i<=n;i++)p[i]=i;
    long long ans=0;int cnt=0;
    for(auto&e:ed){
        int ra=find(e.a),rb=find(e.b);
        if(ra!=rb){p[ra]=rb;ans+=e.w;cnt++;}
    }
    if(cnt<n-1)cout<<"impossible"<<'\n';
    else cout<<ans<<'\n';
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7258（JD154 二色分界）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <queue>
using namespace std;

const int N = 1005;
vector<int> g[N];
int color[N]; // 0未染色 1红 2蓝

bool bfs(int st)
{
    queue<int> q;
    q.push(st);
    color[st] = 1;
    while (!q.empty())
    {
        int u = q.front();
        q.pop();
        for (int v : g[u])
        {
            if (color[v] == 0)
            {
                color[v] = 3 - color[u];
                q.push(v);
            }
            else if (color[v] == color[u])
            {
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

int main()
{
    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i)
    {
        int a, b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        g[a].push_back(b);
        g[b].push_back(a);
    }

    bool ok = true;
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i)
    {
        if (color[i] == 0)
        {
            if (!bfs(i))
            {
                ok = false;
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    if (ok) cout << "Yes\n";
    else cout << "No\n";
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7259（JD155 一剑一鞘）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    int n, V;
    if (cin >> n >> V) {
        vector<int> dp(V + 1, 0);
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            int v, w;
            cin >> v >> w;
            // 01背包必须逆序遍历容量
            for (int j = V; j >= v; --j) {
                dp[j] = max(dp[j], dp[j - v] + w);
            }
        }
        cout << dp[V] << "\n";
    }
    return 0;
}

#include <algorithm>
using namespace std;

        vector<vector<int>> a(n, vector<int>(n, 0));

            for(int j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
                cin >> a[i][j];

            for(int j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {
                a[i][j] += max(a[i+1][j], a[i+1][j+1]);
            }
        }
        cout << a[0][0] << "\n";
    }
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7262（JD158 递增剑意）

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    int n;
    if (cin >> n) {
        vector<int> q;
        for(int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            int x;
            cin >> x;
            // lower_bound 返回第一个大于等于 x 的元素的迭代器
            auto it = lower_bound(q.begin(), q.end(), x);
            if (it == q.end()) {
                q.push_back(x);
            } else {
                *it = x;
            }
        }
        cout << q.size() << "\n";
    }
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7263（JD159 双谱共鸣）

#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;


const int N = 1010;


int dp[N][N];


int main()
{
    int n,m;


    string a,b;


    cin >> n >> m;

    cin >> a;

    cin >> b;



    // 下标从1开始方便处理
    a = " " + a;
    b = " " + b;



    for(int i=1;i<=n;i++)
    {
        for(int j=1;j<=m;j++)
        {
            if(a[i] == b[j])
            {
                dp[i][j] =
                    dp[i-1][j-1] + 1;
            }
            else
            {
                dp[i][j] =
                    max(
                        dp[i-1][j],
                        dp[i][j-1]
                    );
            }
        }
    }



    cout << dp[n][m];


    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7264（JD160 合石成堆）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <climits>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    int n;
    if (cin >> n) {
        vector<int> a(n + 1), sum(n + 1, 0);
        for(int i = 1; i <= n; ++i) {
            cin >> a[i];
            sum[i] = sum[i-1] + a[i]; // 计算前缀和
        }
        
        vector<vector<int>> dp(n + 1, vector<int>(n + 1, 0));
        
        // len 为区间长度，从 2 开始枚举
        for(int len = 2; len <= n; ++len) {
            for(int i = 1; i + len - 1 <= n; ++i) {
                int j = i + len - 1;
                dp[i][j] = INT_MAX;
                // 枚举分割点 k
                for(int k = i; k < j; ++k) {
                    int cost = dp[i][k] + dp[k+1][j] + (sum[j] - sum[i-1]);
                    dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j], cost);
                }
            }
        }
        cout << dp[1][n] << "\n";
    }
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7265（JD161 改字成经）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main()
{
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    vector<string> lines;
    string line;
    // 读取所有输入行
    for (; getline(cin, line);)
    {
        lines.push_back(line);
    }
    // 删除末尾空行
    while (!lines.empty() && lines.back().empty())
    {
        lines.pop_back();
    }

    int n = 0, m = 0;
    string a, b;
    {
        stringstream ss(lines[0]);
        vector<string> t;
        string x;
        while (ss >> x)
        {
            t.push_back(x);
        }

        if (t.size() == 2)
        {
            n = stoi(t[0]);
            m = stoi(t[1]);
            if (lines.size() > 1) a = lines[1];
            if (lines.size() > 2) b = lines[2];
        }
        else
        {
            n = stoi(t[0]);
            if (lines.size() > 1) a = lines[1];
            if (lines.size() > 2) m = stoi(lines[2]);
            if (lines.size() > 3) b = lines[3];
        }
    }

    vector<vector<int>> dp(n + 1, vector<int>(m + 1));
    // 边界初始化
    for (int i = 0; i <= n; ++i)
    {
        dp[i][0] = i;
    }
    for (int j = 0; j <= m; ++j)
    {
        dp[0][j] = j;
    }

    // 编辑距离DP核心
    for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i)
    {
        for (int j = 1; j <= m; ++j)
        {
            dp[i][j] = min(dp[i - 1][j] + 1, dp[i][j - 1] + 1);
            dp[i][j] = min(dp[i][j], dp[i - 1][j - 1] + (a[i - 1] != b[j - 1]));
        }
    }

    cout << dp[n][m] << "\n";
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7266（JD162 雪道寻长）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C ｜ JSON

#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

#define MAXR 105
#define MAXC 105

int R, C;
int h[MAXR][MAXC];
int dp[MAXR][MAXC];
int dx[4] = {-1, 1, 0, 0};
int dy[4] = {0, 0, -1, 1};

int dfs(int x, int y) {
    if (dp[x][y] != -1) return dp[x][y];
    int best = 1;
    for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
        int nx = x + dx[k], ny = y + dy[k];
        if (nx >= 0 && nx < R && ny >= 0 && ny < C && h[nx][ny] < h[x][y]) {
            int val = dfs(nx, ny) + 1;
            if (val > best) best = val;
        }
    }
    return dp[x][y] = best;
}

int main() {
    scanf("%d%d", &R, &C);
    for (int i = 0; i < R; i++)
        for (int j = 0; j < C; j++)
            scanf("%d", &h[i][j]);
    memset(dp, -1, sizeof(dp));
    int ans = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < R; i++)
        for (int j = 0; j < C; j++) {
            int cur = dfs(i, j);
            if (cur > ans) ans = cur;
        }
    printf("%d\n", ans);
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7267（JD163 背包叠甲）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C ｜ JSON

#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

#define MAXV 20005
#define MAXN 1005

int dp[MAXV];
int w[MAXN * 15], v[MAXN * 15], cnt; // 拆分后的物品

int main() {
    int N, V;
    scanf("%d%d", &N, &V);
    cnt = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < N; i++) {
        int vi, wi, s;
        scanf("%d%d%d", &vi, &wi, &s);
        // 二进制拆分
        int k = 1;
        while (k <= s) {
            w[++cnt] = wi * k;
            v[cnt] = vi * k;
            s -= k;
            k <<= 1;
        }
        if (s > 0) {
            w[++cnt] = wi * s;
            v[cnt] = vi * s;
        }
    }
    // 01背包
    memset(dp, 0, sizeof(dp));
    for (int i = 1; i <= cnt; i++) {
        for (int j = V; j >= v[i]; j--) {
            if (dp[j - v[i]] + w[i] > dp[j])
                dp[j] = dp[j - v[i]] + w[i];
        }
    }
    printf("%d\n", dp[V]);
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7268（JD164 背包限重）

// 本解法与“背包叠甲”完全相同，只是数据更大，但数组已开到足够大
// 为了体现区别，这里直接复用代码，但可调整数组大小
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

#define MAXV 20005
#define MAXN 1005

int dp[MAXV];
int w[MAXN * 15], v[MAXN * 15], cnt;

int main() {
    int N, V;
    scanf("%d%d", &N, &V);
    cnt = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < N; i++) {
        int vi, wi, s;
        scanf("%d%d%d", &vi, &wi, &s);
        int k = 1;
        while (k <= s) {
            w[++cnt] = wi * k;
            v[cnt] = vi * k;
            s -= k;
            k <<= 1;
        }
        if (s > 0) {
            w[++cnt] = wi * s;
            v[cnt] = vi * s;
        }
    }
    memset(dp, 0, sizeof(dp));
    for (int i = 1; i <= cnt; i++) {
        for (int j = V; j >= v[i]; j--) {
            if (dp[j - v[i]] + w[i] > dp[j])
                dp[j] = dp[j - v[i]] + w[i];
        }
    }
    printf("%d\n", dp[V]);
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7269（JD165 分组选宝）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C ｜ JSON

#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

#define MAXV 1005
#define MAXN 105

int dp[MAXV];
int v[MAXN], w[MAXN];

int main() {
    int N, V;
    scanf("%d%d", &N, &V);
    memset(dp, 0, sizeof(dp));
    for (int i = 0; i < N; i++) {
        int S;
        scanf("%d", &S);
        for (int j = 0; j < S; j++) {
            scanf("%d%d", &v[j], &w[j]);
        }
        // 逆序容量
        for (int j = V; j >= 0; j--) {
            for (int k = 0; k < S; k++) {
                if (j >= v[k]) {
                    if (dp[j - v[k]] + w[k] > dp[j])
                        dp[j] = dp[j - v[k]] + w[k];
                }
            }
        }
    }
    printf("%d\n", dp[V]);
    return 0;
}

    cin.tie(nullptr);

    vector<int> q;

    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        int x;
        cin >> x;

内部 ID 7271（JD167 整分剑法）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

const int MOD = 1000000007;

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n;
    cin >> n;

    vector<long long> dp(n + 1);
    dp[0] = 1;

    for (int i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
        for (int j = i; j <= n; j++) {
            dp[j] = (dp[j] + dp[j - i]) % MOD;
        }
    }

    cout << dp[n];

    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7273（JD169 棋盘铺阵）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main()
{
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m;
    while (cin >> n >> m && (n || m))
    {
        vector<int> ok(1 << m);
        // 预处理合法单行状态
        for (int sta = 0; sta < (1 << m); sta++)
        {
            int cnt = 0;
            bool good = true;
            for (int i = 0; i < m; i++)
            {
                if (sta >> i & 1)
                {
                    if (cnt & 1)
                    {
                        good = false;
                        break;
                    }
                    cnt = 0;
                }
                else
                {
                    cnt++;
                }
            }
            if (cnt & 1) good = false;
            ok[sta] = good;
        }

        // 预处理合法状态转移
        vector<vector<int>> trans(1 << m);
        for (int a = 0; a < (1 << m); a++)
        {
            for (int b = 0; b < (1 << m); b++)
            {
                if ((a & b) == 0 && ok[a | b])
                {
                    trans[a].push_back(b);
                }
            }
        }

        vector<long long> dp(1 << m), ndp(1 << m);
        dp[0] = 1;
        // 逐行DP
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
        {
            fill(ndp.begin(), ndp.end(), 0);
            for (int a = 0; a < (1 << m); a++)
            {
                if (!dp[a]) continue;
                for (int b : trans[a])
                {
                    ndp[b] += dp[a];
                }
            }
            dp.swap(ndp);
        }
        cout << dp[0] << '\n';
    }
    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7274（JD170 万城巡游）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

const int INF = 1e9;

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n;
    cin >> n;

    vector<vector<int>> g(n, vector<int>(n));

    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
        for (int j = 0; j < n; j++)
            cin >> g[i][j];

    int S = 1 << n;

    vector<vector<int>> dp(S, vector<int>(n, INF));

    dp[1][0] = 0;

    for (int mask = 1; mask < S; mask++) {
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            if (!(mask >> i & 1)) continue;
            if (dp[mask][i] == INF) continue;

            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                if (mask >> j & 1) continue;
                int nxt = mask | (1 << j);
                dp[nxt][j] = min(dp[nxt][j], dp[mask][i] + g[i][j]);
            }
        }
    }

    cout << dp[S - 1][n - 1];

    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7275（JD171 群经改字）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;



int editDistance(string a,string b)
{
    int n=a.size();
    int m=b.size();


    vector<vector<int>> dp(
        n+1,
        vector<int>(m+1)
    );



    // 初始化
    for(int i=0;i<=n;i++)
        dp[i][0]=i;


    for(int j=0;j<=m;j++)
        dp[0][j]=j;



    for(int i=1;i<=n;i++)
    {
        for(int j=1;j<=m;j++)
        {
            int cost;


            if(a[i-1]==b[j-1])
                cost=0;
            else
                cost=1;



            dp[i][j]=min({
                dp[i-1][j]+1,      // 删除
                dp[i][j-1]+1,      // 插入
                dp[i-1][j-1]+cost  // 修改
            });
        }
    }


    return dp[n][m];
}



int main()
{
    int N,M;


    cin >> N >> M;



    vector<string> dict(N);



    for(int i=0;i<N;i++)
        cin >> dict[i];



    while(M--)
    {
        string s;
        int k;


        cin >> s >> k;



        int ans=0;



        for(string word:dict)
        {
            if(editDistance(word,s)<=k)
                ans++;
        }



        cout << ans << endl;
    }



    return 0;
}

内部 ID 7276（JD172 千机棋局）

分数：100.0 ｜ 语言：C++ ｜ JSON

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>

using namespace std;


const int N = 10010;


vector<int> son[N];


int happy[N];


int dp[N][2];


bool hasFather[N];



void dfs(int u)
{
    // 选择u
    dp[u][1]=happy[u];


    // 不选择u
    dp[u][0]=0;



    for(int v:son[u])
    {
        dfs(v);



        // u不出征
        dp[u][0]+=max(
            dp[v][0],
            dp[v][1]
        );



        // u出征
        dp[u][1]+=dp[v][0];
    }
}



int main()
{
    int n;

    cin >> n;



    for(int i=1;i<=n;i++)
    {
        cin >> happy[i];
    }



    for(int i=0;i<n-1;i++)
    {
        int l,k;

        cin >> l >> k;


        son[k].push_back(l);


        hasFather[l]=true;
    }



    int root=1;


    while(hasFather[root])
        root++;



    dfs(root);



    cout << max(
        dp[root][0],
        dp[root][1]
    );


    return 0;
}
